# Scent Box build pack

Everything an AI coding agent needs to rebuild the Scent Box app UI in the Scent Box Clean style for Web (HTML and CSS): the brief, the design tokens, the real code that draws the UI kit and screens and the logo, with a prompt at the top that says how to use it. Generated by Looks Good.

## Part 0: How to use this file (prompt for the agent)

This section is addressed to you, the AI coding agent. Read it first and follow it exactly. It tells you what this file is, which part wins when two disagree, what to do in what order, and what to report when you finish.

**What this file is.** The build pack for Scent Box: App UI, Scent Box Clean style, for Web (HTML and CSS). It holds the brand brief (Part 1), the design tokens (Part 2), the real CSS and reference markup that draw every UI kit component (Part 3) and every screen layout (Part 4), the logo (Part 6) and a checklist you must pass (Part 7). Everything you need is here; do not redraw anything from memory and do not invent values this file does not give.

**Authority order.** When two things in this file disagree, the earlier one wins:

1. Part 1 brief rules: what is allowed, where, and why.
2. Part 2 tokens: the only colours, type faces and spacing values you may use.
3. Part 3 component CSS values (`kit.css`): every colour, size, radius, border, shadow and type setting of every component.
4. Part 3 and Part 4 reference markup: illustrative. It shows structure and intent; class names are Looks Good's own, and the demo copy ("Brand refresh", "Maya Ortiz") is placeholder text.

**Steps.**

1. Save this file in the project root as `BUILD-PACK.md`, and read Part 1 completely before writing code.
2. Create the token file from Part 2 (`tokens.css`: CSS custom properties, light and dark) in the project's stack (plain CSS, Tailwind theme, CSS modules). Keep the token names; every colour in the project comes from them.
3. Build each UI kit component from Part 3 by porting the `kit.css` values exactly into the project's styling system. You may rename the Looks Good class names (`ab-*`, `sb-k-*`, `sb-*`) to the project's own conventions, but keep every value: colours, sizes, radii, borders, shadows, type, spacing and every state (rest, pressed, focus, disabled, open and so on). The selectors are scoped to the kit root described in Part 3; keep the same scoping or fold it into your own. The custom properties `kit.css` reads are resolved in Part 2.
4. Assemble screens from Part 4: each layout names its blocks in order, its rules, and gives the reference markup of the phone screen. Spacing, the 44pt tap target and the type scale come from Part 1 and do not change per screen.
5. Patterns are out of scope for this build: it is an App, and app screens carry no patterns or art.
6. Place the logo from Part 6 at the sizes and positions Part 1 gives.
7. Finish with the Part 7 checklist: go through every item, fix what fails, and do not call the work done until all of it passes.

**Hard rules** (they repeat Part 1 on purpose).

- No colour outside the tokens. No gradient, opacity or tint that is not derived from them as Part 1 says.
- No art and no patterns in app UI: app screens are built from the UI kit only.
- The demo copy in the reference markup ("Brand refresh", "Maya Ortiz" and the like) is placeholder: replace it with the product's real text, but keep the structure and length discipline.
- Keep the 44pt minimum tap target and the phone type scale (sizes, line heights, weights) exactly as Part 1 gives them.
- Match both light and dark exactly as the tokens define them; the dark palette is designed, not inverted.

**Report back** when you finish: (1) the files you created or changed; (2) which Part 7 items pass, and for any that do not, why; (3) every place you could not match a Part 3 value or a Part 1 rule, and what you used instead; (4) any placeholder copy you left in.

## Part 1: Brand brief

The full AI brief for this brand, unchanged apart from its headings being one step deeper. It holds the rules: colours and where each is used, type, shape, states, motion, accessibility and what is allowed where. Where anything in this file conflicts with it, the brief wins.

### Scent Box — build brief
Build the real product from this brief, not a mock-up. Sections are marked Locked, Guided or Free. Locked values are final: use only these colors, fonts, shapes and motion, and where one is missing use the nearest listed token. Guided sections give an intent and a range; stay inside them. Free means design it for this product: layout, screen structure and how many sections a screen has. Screens are composed from the UI kit below, one component for each need; do not invent new component styles, and do not copy the brand board's layout. Target: a phone app in HTML and CSS, laid out in iOS points (1pt = 1 CSS px).
Scent Box: Thoughtful by design. Aesthetic: Scent Box Clean (Quiet · Tiled · Soft; Muji and Aesop shop shelves / printed perfume-house swatch cards / Apple Notes and Things / Swiss price lists). Soft warm-grey tiles in an even bento grid on a quiet ground, with nothing ruled or shadowed, so scent colour is the only colour. Let the scent be the colour.
Product: Scent Box. A personal fragrance collection tracker: what I own, what I want and what I liked, with each scent's notes and accords in its own colour.

#### App UI rules (Guided)
Build every screen from the UI kit only. The brand shows up through colour, type, corners, edges, shadow and how each component is drawn. Never put the board's hero illustration, deck art, patterns, textures, decorative bands or header art into app screens. The logo appears at icon size in the top bar or on the welcome screen, nowhere else. Images appear only when they are content (a user photo, a product photo). Never introduce a colour that is not in the palette: destructive and status colours come from it too.
The brand board, deck and landing page show art for the brand's identity and marketing; none of it belongs in the app.
Screens are composed from the UI kit and the phone layout below: pick the component for the need, set text in the type scale, and leave the rest to the tokens.
Focus rings appear on keyboard focus only (`:focus-visible` on the web, the system focus on SwiftUI), never on tap or click; disabled controls take no focus and no press response.

##### How Scent Box Clean shows up in the app
- Corners: buttons pill, fields pill, cards 18pt, badges pill.
- Surfaces: card --kit-primary-2 at 14% over --canvas; list rows unfilled; bottom sheet --canvas.
- Type: welcome title DM Sans (display) 400, tracking -0.01em; section labels DM Sans (display) 400, uppercase, tracking +0.14em; button labels DM Sans (display) 700.
- Primary and selection: the primary button is --ink with a --canvas label; the selected segment is --ab-pair-a-tint; the selected tab shows a 16x3pt --primary pill, near the bottom of the box, centred.
- Signature details: toggle: a 21pt mark drawn as --ab-muted with a diagonal --on-primary at 70% stroke, vertically centred in the box, at the left; pie menu: a 152pt --ink at 3% dot with a 1pt solid --line edge, vertically centred in the box, centred; skeleton screen: a smooth fade of --canvas at 55% at 90° to transparent (a soft gloss, no hard line).

Where this aesthetic's colour rules and a component description disagree, the colour rules win.
Palette colours no app component draws (brand board and app icon only; they stay in `:root`): `--secondary` #ece7de, `--accent-ink` #9a7b4f, `--sec-tint` #f4f3ef / #403d3a, `--kit-stepper` #3b3a38.
- Primary budget: `--primary` appears only where the UI kit draws it (Text field, Search field, Checkbox, Radio, Toggle, Divider and section label, Tab bar, Tabs, Combo box, Listbox, Knob, Date picker, Navigation bar, Anchor links, Ribbon, Pie menu), never as extra fills, text or rules. Selection, toggles, tabs and focus are drawn as their kit rows say, not in extra primary.
- Highlight word: use the brand's tagline unchanged as the welcome title (never reword it) and highlight its last word together with any full stop, wrapped in one span with `white-space: nowrap`; draw it as the Built Welcome screen does: text --accent, a gradient from undefined to undefined (top to bottom).
- Text colours are the ones each UI kit row states; no other colours, tints or gradients on text.

#### UI kit (Locked)
Measured from the UI kit tab as Scent Box Clean draws it. Every control and container in the product is one of these components, drawn exactly as stated. Colours are tokens from Colors (a plain hex or rgba() is a fixed value the kit draws); "`--x` at N%" means `color-mix(in srgb, var(--x) N%, transparent)` and "at N% over `--canvas`" means `color-mix(in srgb, var(--x) N%, var(--canvas))`; sizes are points at the 390 x 844 reference (1pt = 1 CSS px); padding is CSS shorthand; exact shadow values are the `--shadow-*` tokens; (display) and (body) name the brand faces. "Other states" lists only what changes from the first state.

| Bottom sheet | A task or set of choices that slides up from the bottom. | --canvas | --ink, DM Sans (display) 22pt 400, tracking -0.02em | no edge; square corners | padding 10pt 20pt 20pt 20pt | grabber: fill --line, pill, 5pt high | one state |
| Side sheet | Supporting content or filters that slide in from the side edge. | --canvas | --ink, DM Sans (display) 22pt 400, tracking -0.02em | no edge; square corners | padding 8pt 16pt 16pt 16pt | scrim: fill --ink at 6% over --canvas, 16pt corners; scrim: a --ink at 22% sheet covering the whole box | one state |
| Popup | A small panel attached to the control that opened it. | --canvas | --ink, DM Sans (display) 17pt 400, tracking -0.02em | no edge; square corners | padding 20pt | trailing mark at the end of the label: a 14pt chip filled --canvas, holding nothing | one state |
| Popup tip | A one-time hint pointing at a feature, with a way to dismiss it. | --canvas | --ink, DM Sans (display) 17pt 400, tracking -0.02em | no edge; square corners | padding 20pt | trailing mark at the end of the label: a 14pt chip filled --canvas, holding nothing; action: --ink, DM Sans (display) 14pt 700, uppercase | one state |
| Tooltip | A short label for a control, shown on hover, focus or long press. | --ink | --canvas, DM Sans (display) 13pt 400 | no edge; 9pt corners | padding 6pt 10pt | trailing mark at the end of the label: a 14pt chip filled --ink, holding nothing | one state |
| Lightbox | One image enlarged over a dark layer, with close and next. | --canvas | --ink, DM Sans (display) 13pt 400 | no edge; square corners | padding 56pt 56pt 48pt 56pt | image: fill --ink at 86% over --canvas, 12pt corners | one state |

#### Product screens (Guided)
These are Scent Box's own screens, from its product sheet. What gets checked is the content ranking (1 is the most important), where the style's signature lands, and the fold budget; the layout itself is designed for the product from the kit's components, with no pixel positions given. Comps of these screens are viewable in Looks Good only.
Platform: Mac. Each screen is a Mac app window, 1280x800 (comps are 2560x1600 at 2x); content taller than the window is drawn in full with a fold line at 800.

##### Collection
- Job: See the whole shelf at a glance, filter it, and open any fragrance.
- Primary action: Add fragrance
- Chrome: Toolbar in the title bar
- Content, ranked:
  1. Large title "Scent Box".
  2. Segmented control "" with options "All 37", "Owned 34", "Interested 0", "Liked 3", "All 37" selected.
  3. Input field "Search", placeholder "Search name, house or note".
  4. Text link "Filter · 18 houses".
  5. The one primary button "Add fragrance".
  6. Stat row "" with Full bottles: 7; Travel and decants: 3; Samples: 24.
  7. Stat row "" with Santa Maria Novella: Tabacco Toscano ★★★★☆; Santa Maria Novella: Vaniglia ★★★★★; Givenchy: Pi ★★★★☆; Scents of Wood: Praline in Maple 50 mL ★★★☆☆; Le Labo: Thé Noir 29 sample; d'Annam: Moonlight Samurai sample.
- Signature placement: soft tiles on a quiet ground.
- Fold budget: Title, tabs, search, the stats tiles and the first fragrance tiles fit in the 800 px window.
- States:
  - Empty: No fragrances yet. One line and Add fragrance as the only action.
  - Loading: none; the data is on the device.
  - Error: none.
  - First run: none.

##### Fragrance detail
- Job: Read everything about one fragrance: how it smells, its accords and notes, and my own rating and notes.
- Primary action: Save
- Chrome: Toolbar in the title bar
- Content, ranked:
  1. Card "Tabacco Toscano", with "Santa Maria Novella · Full bottle · Owned", body "Dry tobacco leaf with warm spice over a soft leather base.".
  2. The one primary button "Save".
  3. Stat row "" with Concentration: EDP; Year: 2003.
  4. Stat row "" with tobacco: 100; warm spicy: 82; leather: 68.
  5. Card "Notes", with "Top: Bergamot · Lemon · Pink pepper", body "Heart: Tobacco leaf · Spices · Honey · Base: Leather · Amber · Vanilla".
  6. Card "★★★★☆", with "My rating · Owned · Full bottle".
  7. Card "My notes", body "Best in autumn. Two sprays is plenty; it lasts all day on a jacket.".
- Signature placement: soft tiles; scent colour is the only strong colour.
- Fold budget: Hero, facts, accord tiles and notes fit above the fold.
- States:
  - Empty: Unrated fragrance shows five empty stars.
  - Loading: none.
  - Error: none.
  - First run: none.

##### Add fragrance
- Job: Add a new fragrance in a few fields and save it to the shelf.
- Primary action: Save fragrance
- Chrome: Toolbar in the title bar
- Content, ranked:
  1. Large title "Add fragrance".
  2. Input field "House", placeholder "Santa Maria Novella".
  3. Input field "Name", placeholder "Tabacco Toscano".
  4. Segmented control "" with options "Owned", "Interested", "Liked", "Owned" selected.
  5. Segmented control "" with options "Full bottle", "Travel", "Decant", "Sample", "Full bottle" selected.
  6. Input field "Accords", placeholder "tobacco, warm spicy, leather".
  7. The one primary button "Save fragrance".
- Signature placement: soft filled fields on a quiet ground.
- Fold budget: Every field and the action fit in the window.
- States:
  - Empty: All fields blank; Save fragrance is disabled until House and Name are filled.
  - Loading: none.
  - Error: House and Name are required; the missing field gets a plain line under it.
  - First run: none.

#### Phone layout and type (Guided)
| Role | Face | Size pt | Line | Weight |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Display | DM Sans (display), tracking -0.01em | 34 | 41 | 400 |
| Eyebrow | DM Sans (display), uppercase, tracking +0.1em | 13 | 18 | 400 |
| Label | DM Sans (display), uppercase, tracking +0.14em | 13 | 18 | 400 |
| Large title | DM Sans (display), tracking -0.02em | 34 | 41 | 400 |
| Title | DM Sans (display), tracking -0.02em | 28 | 34 | 400 |
| Title 2 | DM Sans (display), tracking -0.02em | 22 | 28 | 400 |
| Headline | DM Sans (display) | 17 | 22 | 400 |
| Body | DM Sans (display) | 17 | 24 | 400 |
| Callout | DM Sans (display) | 16 | 22 | 400 |
| Subhead | DM Sans (display) | 15 | 20 | 400 |
| Footnote | DM Sans (display) | 13 | 18 | 400 |
| Caption | DM Sans (display) | 12 | 16 | 700 |
| Tab label | DM Sans (display) | 10 | 12 | 700 selected, 400 unselected |
- Phone chrome: the status bar takes 54pt at the top and the home indicator 34pt at the bottom; nothing tappable sits in either. The top bar is 44pt high, under the status bar. At rest it shows only the back button and icon actions, with the Large title below it in the content; the title appears in the top bar (Headline size) only once the Large title has scrolled under it.
- Title colours: welcome title --ink, screen titles --ink, eyebrows --ab-muted.
- Layout: 20pt side margins on every screen. Spacing comes from 4, 8, 12, 16, 20, 24, 28, 32, 40, 48pt only; 28pt between sections. Sizes: buttons 52pt, fields 48pt, list rows 52pt with one line and 60pt with a detail line, card insides as the Home card in the sample screens, tab bar 83pt, nothing tappable under 44pt.
- Support Dynamic Type; never let a word split or a label truncate; one Large title per screen.

#### Light and dark (Locked)
The default look is light, as the sample screens draw it: `color-scheme: light` and the light values on `:root`. Dark is used only when the system asks for it (`[data-theme="dark"]`, set from `prefers-color-scheme: dark` by the script under Tokens). The dark values are designed, not inverted. Text on the canvas uses `--ink` / `--muted`; text on cards and paper uses `--card-ink` / `--card-muted`, with each mode's value from the Colors table.

#### States (Locked)
| State | Colour | Visual |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Pressed and disabled | as each UI kit row states | The "Other states" column of the UI kit is the one definition; nothing else changes on press. Disabled controls take no press response. |
| Focus | `--primary` where it holds 3:1 on the canvas, else `--ink` | Keyboard focus on any control: a 2pt ring with a 2pt gap, following the control's corners; exactly as the "focus" state of the Primary and Secondary button rows in the UI kit draws it. Shown on keyboard focus only (`:focus-visible` on the web), never on tap or click; never removed. Text fields show their own focus state from the UI kit instead. |
| Empty | `--ink` headline, `--muted` copy | The Empty state row of the UI kit. |
| Loading | `--line` track, `--primary` fill | Indeterminate bar, or skeleton blocks in `--line` easing between 100% and 60% opacity every 1.2 s. Show it after 300 ms of waiting, with a text label for screen readers. |
| Error | `--error` | The Text field row's error state, with the message under the field. |

#### Motion (Guided)
- Durations: fast 100 ms (press, toggle), base 160 ms (color, selection, shadow), slow 240 ms (sheets, page changes). Easing: ease-out `cubic-bezier(0.2, 0.8, 0.2, 1)`.
- Reduce Motion: Replace slides and fades with an instant state swap; colour changes stay. State changes stay; only the movement goes. Nothing else animates idly or loops except loading indicators.

#### Accessibility (Locked)
- Contrast kept (light | dark): ink on canvas 16.9 | 16.1 (AAA); muted on canvas 4.7 | 6.6 (AA); on-primary on primary 14.0 | 14.0 (AAA); on-accent on accent 4.7 | 4.7 (AA); error on canvas 16.9 | 16.1 (AAA). Keep every text pair at 4.5:1 or better (3:1 for the accent-ink highlight word); do not swap tokens in a way that breaks them.
- Every tappable target is at least 44x44 CSS px even when the visible shape is smaller, with 8pt between neighbours. Text scales: sizes in rem, no fixed-height text boxes; layouts reflow at the largest accessibility size (stack, wrap, never truncate a primary label).
- Reduce Transparency (`prefers-reduced-transparency`): translucent fills in the kit (overlay, skeleton screen) become opaque. Increase Contrast (`prefers-contrast: more`): Hairlines become 2pt `--ink`, `--muted` text becomes `--ink`, tints get a 1pt `--ink` edge.
- Reduce Motion (`prefers-reduced-motion`): see Motion. Never color alone: selection, status and errors also use an icon, text or shape; icon-only buttons carry an accessibility label.

#### Icons (Locked)
1.5px strokes, round caps, 20px; the selected icon is ink with a 1px rule beneath. Web: inline SVG on a 24px grid, `currentColor`.
- Sizes as the sample screens draw them: 24pt tab bar icons over their 10pt labels; 20pt icons in icon buttons, centred in a 44x44pt hit area; 18pt icons inside the 32pt feature-row chips; 16pt chevrons in the trailing slot of rows, never a text glyph (no ›, > or →); 24pt anywhere else.
- Glyphs the kit uses (draw each once, same stroke): back, more, notifications (bell), search, chevron right, chevron down, check, plus, info, success, warning, error, inbox (empty state), home, list, heart, user.

#### Fonts to bundle (Locked)
- DM Sans: 400 (Display, Eyebrow, Label, Large title, Title, Title 2, Headline, Body, Callout, Subhead, Footnote, UI kit), 400 italic (UI kit), 700 (Caption, Tab label, UI kit).
- Geist Mono: 600 (UI kit).
- System faces, no file to bundle: -apple-system.
- Bundle exactly these weights and no others; the type scale uses no more. Web: https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=DM+Sans:wght@400;700&family=Geist+Mono:wght@600&display=swap

#### Signature and product imagery (Guided)
- Embedded product screenshots, app icons and product imagery keep their own colours. They are exempt from the canvas and colour coverage budget and from the accent and primary limits; the studio frame around them (surfaces, captions, borders) follows the brand rules.
- Coverage and whitespace targets apply to the brand's own surfaces, not to product pages that showcase a product's own visual system.

#### Do and don't (Guided)
- Do: Mark the chosen tab or segment with a tint pill and ink text; keep the one ink-filled action per view.
- Do: Drop eyebrow labels where the tile already says what it is; fewer words, fewer visible controls.
- Don't: Use hairline rules between rows, chevrons on tiles, or borders around tiles.
- Don't: Colour the interface itself: tiles, tabs and buttons stay ink and warm grey.
- Don't: Fill a selected tab or segment with solid black.

#### Tokens (CSS) (Locked)

```css
  --font-geist-mono: "Geist Mono", ui-monospace, monospace;
  --text-display: 34px; --text-eyebrow: 13px; --text-label: 13px; --text-large-title: 34px; --text-title: 28px; --text-title-2: 22px; --text-headline: 17px; --text-body: 17px; --text-callout: 16px; --text-subhead: 15px; --text-footnote: 13px; --text-caption: 12px; --text-tab-label: 10px;
  --space-4: 4px; --space-8: 8px; --space-12: 12px; --space-16: 16px; --space-20: 20px; --space-24: 24px; --space-28: 28px; --space-32: 32px; --space-40: 40px; --space-48: 48px;

}
:root[data-theme="dark"] {
  color-scheme: dark;
  --canvas: #1f1d1b; --ink: #fbfaf7; --muted: #a3a29f;
  --line: #403e3c; --pri-tint: #211f1c; --error: #fbfaf7;
  --on-error: #1f1d1b; --kit-primary-2: #fbfaf7; --kit-secondary: #c2c1be;
  --kit-field: #fbfaf7; --kit-field-2: #ff7b72; --ab-muted: #acaba8;
  --kit-toggle: #8b8885; --ab-pair-a-tint: rgba(47,43,39,0.5); --kit-list: #403e3c;
  --success-icon: #ece7de; --warning-icon: #9a7b4f; --primary-text: #8b8885;
  --kit-dropdown-menu: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0); --kit-toast-text: #8f8e8d; --kit-stepper-text: #b1b0af;
  --card-ink: #fbfaf7; --card-muted: #acaba8; --sec-tint: #403d3a;
}
@media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) { :root { --dur-fast: 0ms; --dur-base: 0ms; --dur-slow: 0ms; } }
```
Follow the system appearance:
```js
const darkMq = matchMedia('(prefers-color-scheme: dark)');
const applyTheme = () => { document.documentElement.dataset.theme = darkMq.matches ? 'dark' : 'light'; };
applyTheme(); darkMq.addEventListener('change', applyTheme);
```

#### App icon (Locked)
A warm-white rounded tile with one thin ink outline of a bottle and a single tinted drop. Deliver a 1024x1024 master without transparency or pre-rounded corners (the system masks it), a dark variant on `#1f1d1b` and a tinted variant (glyph only, one color), and derive every other size from the master. Web: also a 512px maskable icon and a 32px favicon from the same master.

## Part 2: Design tokens

These are the brand's tokens: use them as given, and use nothing else for colour, type or spacing. The first block is the same file "Save + files" writes; the last block holds the values of every custom property the Part 3 CSS reads, resolved on the kit root, so every `var(--x)` in Part 3 has a value here.

### tokens.css

```css
  --row: 52px; --row-detail: 60px;
  --radius-control: 999px; --radius-field: 999px; --radius-card: 18px; --hit: 44px;
  --shadow-toast: color-mix(in srgb, var(--black) 5%, transparent) 0 1px 2px 0, color-mix(in srgb, var(--black) 20%, transparent) 0 8px 20px -8px;
  --dur-fast: 100ms; --dur-base: 160ms; --dur-slow: 240ms; --ease: cubic-bezier(0.2, 0.8, 0.2, 1);
}
:root[data-theme="dark"] {
  color-scheme: dark;
  --canvas: #1f1d1b; --ink: #fbfaf7; --muted: #a3a29f;
  --line: #403e3c; --pri-tint: #211f1c; --error: #fbfaf7;
  --on-error: #1f1d1b; --kit-primary-2: #fbfaf7; --kit-secondary: #c2c1be;
  --kit-field: #fbfaf7; --kit-field-2: #ff7b72; --ab-muted: #acaba8;
  --kit-toggle: #8b8885; --ab-pair-a-tint: rgba(47,43,39,0.5); --kit-list: #403e3c;
  --success-icon: #ece7de; --warning-icon: #9a7b4f; --primary-text: #8b8885;
  --kit-dropdown-menu: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0); --kit-toast-text: #8f8e8d; --kit-stepper-text: #b1b0af;
  --card-ink: #fbfaf7; --card-muted: #acaba8; --sec-tint: #403d3a;
}
@media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) { :root { --dur-fast: 0ms; --dur-base: 0ms; --dur-slow: 0ms; } }
```

### Resolved custom properties read by the kit CSS

Set these on the kit root (the element carrying class `ab sb-ab`) before the Part 3 CSS applies. The first block is the light look; the second lists only the values that differ in dark, scoped by `data-mode`.

```css

  --ab-accent: #9a7b4f;
  --ab-accent-ink: #9a7b4f;
  --ab-accent-mark: transparent;
  --ab-canvas: #f6f5f2;
  --ab-display: 1;

  --ab-ink: #161412;
  --ab-line: #d4d3d0;
  --ab-muted: #5a5856;
  --ab-on-accent: #141414;
  --ab-on-primary: #ffffff;
  --ab-on-secondary: #141414;
  --ab-pair-a-tint: rgba(47,43,39,0.16);
  --ab-pri-tint: #deddda;
  --ab-primary: #2f2b27;
  --ab-primary-text: #2f2b27;
  --ab-sec-tint: #f4f3ef;

  --k-body: 17px;
  --k-body-lh: 24px;

  --k-callout-lh: 22px;

  --k-caption-lh: 16px;
  --k-cardpad: 20px;

  --k-err-ico: #161412;
  --k-field: 48px;
  --k-footnote: 13px;
  --k-footnote-lh: 18px;
  --k-gap: 28px;
  --k-headline: 17px;
  --k-headline-lh: 22px;
  --k-info: #2f2b27;
  --k-info-ico: #2f2b27;
  --k-large-title: 34px;
  --k-large-title-lh: 41px;
  --k-margin: 20px;
  --k-muted: color-mix(in srgb, #161412 28%, #5a5856);
  --k-ok: #ece7de;
  --k-ok-ico: #817e78;
  --k-pri-on-tint: #2f2b27;
  --k-pri-text: #2f2b27;
  --k-r: min(999px, 16px);
  --k-row: 52px;
  --k-subhead: 15px;
  --k-subhead-lh: 20px;
  --k-tab: 83px;

  --k-title-2: 22px;
  --k-title-2-lh: 28px;
  --k-title-lh: 34px;
  --k-ui: "DM Sans", -apple-system, sans-serif;
  --k-warn: #9a7b4f;
  --k-warn-ico: #93764c;
  --mv-on-press: #ffffff;
  --mv-press: #282521;
```
