```css
  --sb-r: 999px;
  --sb-ui-ff: "DM Sans", -apple-system, sans-serif;
  --sb-ui-ls: 0;
  --sb-ui-tt: none;
}
```

```css
.ab.sb-ab[data-mode="dark"] {
  --ab-canvas: #1f1d1b;
  --ab-ink: #fbfaf7;
  --ab-line: #403e3c;
  --ab-muted: #acaba8;
  --ab-pair-a-tint: rgba(47,43,39,0.5);
  --ab-pri-tint: #211f1c;
  --ab-primary-text: #fbfaf7;
  --ab-sec-tint: #403d3a;
  --k-err: #fbfaf7;
  --k-err-ico: #fbfaf7;
  --k-info-ico: #767370;
  --k-muted: color-mix(in srgb, #fbfaf7 28%, #acaba8);
  --k-ok-ico: #ece7de;
  --k-pri-on-tint: #8b8885;
  --k-pri-text: #8b8885;
  --k-warn-ico: #9a7b4f;
  --rl-on-rim: #8b8885;
  --sb-danger: #fbfaf7;
  --sb-danger-on: #1f1d1b;
  --sb-danger-text: #fbfaf7;
  --sb-focus-auto: #fbfaf7;
}
```

Set per element (in the markup's inline `style` or by a rule inside `kit.css`, not on the root): `--h`, `--k-inset`, `--k-picked`, `--pr`, `--t`, `--tone`, `--tone-ico`, `--v`, `--wr`.

Optional hooks this style does not set (each `var()` in `kit.css` carries its own fallback, so leave them unset): `--ab-pair-b`, `--k-arrow-edge`, `--k-callout-edge-w`, `--k-callout-fill`, `--k-callout-r`, `--k-callout-shadow`, `--k-dis-label`, `--k-sheet-r`, `--k-sheet-shadow`, `--mv-hair`, `--mv-hair-soft`, `--mv-hair-strong`, `--rl-hi`, `--rl-lo`, `--sb-focus`.

## Part 3: UI kit source

First comes `kit.css`: every CSS rule that draws the UI kit in this style, in cascade order, grouped by the file it came from. Then each component, with what it is for, its iOS treatment, its states and the reference markup of every state. Port the CSS values exactly; treat the markup as illustrative.

### Kit root

Every selector in `kit.css` is scoped to this root. Rename or fold the scope into your own, but keep the same attributes' meaning (`data-ae` is the style, `data-mode` is light or dark).

```html
<section class="ab sb-ab sb-window sb-kit" data-ae="scent-box-clean" data-mode="light" data-accent-text="ok" data-hero="split" data-palette="swatches" data-sections="two-col" data-highlight="ink" data-states="chip" data-subcopy="on" data-hcls="geometric" data-bcls="geometric" data-sb-danger="ink" data-k-field="box" data-ab-hs0="1" data-ab-hs-fit="1">
  <!-- a component's reference markup goes here -->
</section>
```

### kit.css

514 rules from 4 files: `baseline`, `css/aesthetic-board.css`, `css/see-built.css`, `inline <style>`.

```css
/* ===== baseline (from css/styles.css) ===== */
*,
::before,
::after {
  box-sizing: border-box;
}

@media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) {
  *,
  ::before,
  ::after {
    animation-duration: 0.001ms !important;
    animation-iteration-count: 1 !important;
    transition-duration: 0.001ms !important;
  }
}

/* ===== css/aesthetic-board.css ===== */
.ab {
  container: ab / inline-size;
  position: relative;
  isolation: isolate;
  overflow: hidden;
  background: var(--ab-canvas);
  color: var(--ab-ink);
  font-family: var(--ab-font-ui, var(--ab-font-b));
  --ab-pad: clamp(20px, 4.4cqw, 44px);
  font-size: 14px;
  font-weight: 400;
  line-height: 1.5;
  letter-spacing: 0px;
  text-transform: none;
  text-align: left;
  border-radius: 20px;
  -webkit-font-smoothing: antialiased;
  font-synthesis: none;
}

.ab *,
.ab ::before,
.ab ::after {
  box-sizing: border-box;
}

:where(.ab) button,
:where(.ab) input {
  font: inherit;
  color: inherit;
}

.ab h2,
.ab h3,
.ab p,
.ab ul {
  margin: 0px;
  padding: 0px;
}

.ab i {
  font-style: normal;
}

.ab-brand {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 10px;
  min-width: 0px;
  font-family: var(--ab-font-h);
  font-weight: 700;
  letter-spacing: -0.02em;
  font-size: clamp(15px, 2cqw, 19px);
}

.ab-wordmark {
  overflow: hidden;
  text-overflow: ellipsis;
  white-space: nowrap;
}

.ab:not(.ab-slide) .ab-wordmark {
  overflow: visible;
  text-overflow: clip;
  white-space: normal;
  overflow-wrap: anywhere;
}

.ab-mark {
  flex: 0 0 auto;
  width: 28px;
  height: 28px;
  display: grid;
  place-items: center;
  border-radius: 8px;
  background: var(--ab-ink);
  color: var(--ab-canvas);
  font: 700 14px/1 var(--ab-font-h);
}

.ab-rule {
  height: 1px;
  background: var(--ab-line);
}

.ab-label,
.ab-fieldlabel {
  font-family: var(--ab-font-label, var(--ab-font-ui, var(--ab-font-b)));
}

.ab-labelrow {
  display: flex;
  justify-content: space-between;
  align-items: baseline;
  gap: 12px;
  margin-bottom: 16px;
}

.ab-label {
  font-size: 11px;
  letter-spacing: 0.14em;
  text-transform: uppercase;
  color: var(--ab-muted);
}

.ab-headline {
  font-family: var(--ab-font-h);
  font-weight: 600;
  line-height: 1;
  letter-spacing: -0.03em;
  font-size: calc(clamp(32px, 7.4cqw, 70px) * var(--ab-hl-scale, 1) * var(--ab-hs, 1) * var(--ab-display, 1));
  text-wrap: balance;
  overflow-wrap: normal;
}

.ab[data-ae] .ab-headline:not([data-wrap-last-resort]) {
  overflow-wrap: normal;
}

.ab-hl-glue {
  white-space: nowrap;
}

.ab-subcopy {
  max-width: 36em;
  text-wrap: pretty;
  font-family: var(--ab-font-b);
  font-size: clamp(13.5px, 1.5cqw, 16px);
  line-height: 1.5;
  color: var(--ab-muted);
  margin-top: clamp(14px, 2cqw, 22px) !important;
}

.ab:where([data-highlight="ink"]) .ab-hl {
  color: var(--ab-accent-ink);
  background-image: linear-gradient(transparent 62%, var(--ab-accent-mark, transparent) 62%, var(--ab-accent-mark, transparent) 94%, transparent 94%);
  -webkit-box-decoration-break: clone;
  box-decoration-break: clone;
}

.ab-card {
  padding: clamp(18px, 2.8cqw, 26px);
  border-radius: 16px;
}

.ab-seg {
  display: flex;
  gap: 4px;
  padding: 5px;
  border-radius: 12px;
}

.ab-seg-btn {
  flex: 1 1 0%;
  min-height: 36px;
  padding: 8px 6px;
  border: 0px;
  background: transparent;
  border-radius: 9px;
  font-size: 12px;
  color: var(--ab-muted);
  cursor: pointer;
}

.ab-seg-btn[aria-pressed="true"] {
  color: var(--ab-ink);
  font-weight: 600;
}

.ab-card-title {
  font-family: var(--ab-font-h);
  font-weight: 600;
  font-size: clamp(21px, 2.7cqw, 27px);
  line-height: 1.12;
  letter-spacing: -0.02em;
  margin: 24px 0px 8px !important;
}

.ab-card-body {
  font-family: var(--ab-font-b);
  font-size: 13px;
  color: var(--ab-muted);
  margin-bottom: 22px !important;
}

.ab-cta {
  width: 100%;
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: space-between;
  gap: 10px;
  padding: 13px 16px;
  border: 0px;
  border-radius: 10px;
  cursor: pointer;
  background: var(--ab-primary);
  color: var(--ab-on-primary);
  font-size: 13.5px;
  font-weight: 600;
}

.ab-cta-arrow::before {
  content: "↗";
}

.ab-fieldlabel {
  display: block;
  margin-bottom: 10px;
  font-size: 12px;
}

.ab .ab-input {
  display: block;
  width: 100%;
  height: auto;
  margin-bottom: 18px;
  padding: 13px 15px;
  border: 1px solid var(--ab-line);
  border-radius: 10px;
  background: transparent;
  color: inherit;
  font-family: inherit;
  font-size: 15px;
  font-weight: inherit;
  text-transform: none;
  letter-spacing: 0px;
  outline: none;
}

.ab[data-ae] .ab-input:focus-visible {
  outline: 2px solid var(--ab-ink);
  outline-offset: 2px;
}

.ab-toggle {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: space-between;
  gap: 12px;
  min-height: 44px;
  font-size: 13px;
  cursor: pointer;
}

.ab-switch {
  appearance: none;
  position: relative;
  flex: 0 0 auto;
  margin: 0px;
  width: 50px;
  height: 29px;
  border-radius: 20px;
  cursor: pointer;
  background: var(--ab-line);
  transition: background 0.18s;
}

.ab-switch::before {
  content: "";
  position: absolute;
  left: 4px;
  top: 4px;
  width: 21px;
  height: 21px;
  border-radius: 50%;
  background: var(--ab-canvas);
  transition: transform 0.18s;
}

.ab-switch:checked {
  background: var(--ab-primary);
}

.ab-switch:checked::before {
  transform: translateX(21px);
}

.ab-list {
  list-style: none;
}

.ab-row {
  display: grid;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 4px clamp(10px, 2cqw, 20px);
  padding: 13px 2px;
  grid-template-columns: 2.6ch minmax(0px, 1fr) auto minmax(60px, 150px) 4.5ch;
  border-top: 1px solid var(--ab-line);
  font-size: 13px;
}

.ab-row:last-child {
  border-bottom: 1px solid var(--ab-line);
}

.ab-row-bar {
  height: 6px;
  border-radius: 3px;
  background: var(--ab-line);
  overflow: hidden;
}

.ab-row-bar i {
  display: block;
  height: 100%;
  background: var(--ab-secondary);
}

.ab-fit {
  display: inline-block;
  padding: 2px 9px;
  border: 1px solid var(--ab-line);
  border-radius: 999px;
  font-family: var(--ab-font-label, var(--ab-font-ui, var(--ab-font-b)));
  font-size: 11px;
  font-weight: 600;
  line-height: 1.5;
  white-space: nowrap;
  letter-spacing: 0px;
  text-transform: none;
  color: var(--ab-ink);
}

.ab-fit[data-fit="match"] {
  background: var(--ab-sec-tint);
  border-color: transparent;
  color: var(--ab-ink);
}

.ab-type-apply {
  padding: 10px 16px;
  border: 1px solid var(--ab-ink);
  border-radius: 10px;
  background: transparent;
  color: var(--ab-ink);
  font-size: 13px;
  font-weight: 600;
  cursor: pointer;
  white-space: nowrap;
}

.ab-type-apply:hover {
  background: var(--ab-pri-tint);
}

.ab-type-apply:focus-visible {
  outline: 2px solid var(--ab-ink);
  outline-offset: 2px;
}

@container ab (max-width: 600px) {
  .ab-headline {
    font-size: calc(clamp(30px, 10.5cqw, 52px) * var(--ab-hl-scale, 1) * var(--ab-hs, 1) * var(--ab-display, 1));
  }

  .ab-brand {
    flex: 1 1 auto;
  }

  .ab-labelrow {
    flex-wrap: wrap;
    row-gap: 4px;
  }

  .ab-labelrow > .ab-label:last-child:not(:first-child) {
    white-space: nowrap;
  }

  .ab-row {
    grid-template-columns: 2.6ch minmax(0px, 1fr) 4.5ch;
    row-gap: 6px;
  }

  .ab-row-bar {
    display: none;
  }
}

@media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) {
  .ab *,
  .ab ::before,
  .ab ::after {
    transition: none !important;
    animation: auto ease 0s 1 normal none running none !important;
  }
}

/* ===== css/see-built.css ===== */
.sb-single > .sb-window {
  min-height: 100%;
}

:is(.ab.sb-ab, .sb-gen) {
  --sb-danger: #c62f2f;
  --sb-danger-line: rgba(198, 47, 47, 0.35);
  --sb-danger-on: #fff;
}

:is(.ab.sb-ab, .sb-gen) {
  --sb-danger-text: color-mix(in srgb, var(--sb-danger) 62%, var(--ab-ink, #111));
}

:is(.ab.sb-ab, .sb-gen)[data-mode="dark"] {
  --sb-danger: #ff7b72;
  --sb-danger-line: rgba(255, 123, 114, 0.4);
  --sb-danger-on: #1a0505;
}

:is(.ab.sb-ab, .sb-gen) .sb-ico {
  display: block;
  flex: 0 0 auto;
}

:is(.ab.sb-ab, .sb-gen) :is(button, a) {
  -webkit-tap-highlight-color: transparent;
}

.ab.sb-ab.sb-window {
  min-height: 100%;
}

.sb-search {
  position: relative;
  width: clamp(180px, 24cqw, 260px);
}

.sb-search > .sb-ico {
  position: absolute;
  left: 12px;
  top: 50%;
  translate: 0px -50%;
  z-index: 1;
  color: color-mix(in srgb, var(--ab-ink) 28%, var(--ab-muted));
  pointer-events: none;
}

:is(.ab.sb-ab, .sb-gen) .sb-search .ab-input {
  margin: 0px;
  padding-top: 10px;
  padding-bottom: 10px;
  padding-left: 36px;
  font-size: 13px;
}

:is(.ab.sb-ab, .sb-gen) .ab-type-apply.sb-icon-btn {
  position: relative;
  display: grid;
  place-items: center;
  width: 42px;
  height: 42px;
  padding: 0px;
  flex: 0 0 auto;
}

.sb-badge {
  position: absolute;
  top: 8px;
  right: 9px;
  width: 8px;
  height: 8px;
  border-radius: 50%;
  background: var(--ab-accent);
  box-shadow: 0 0 0 2px var(--ab-canvas);
}

:is(.ab.sb-ab, .sb-gen) .ab-row[tabindex] {
  cursor: pointer;
}

:is(.ab.sb-ab, .sb-gen) .ab-row[tabindex]:focus-visible {
  outline: 2px solid var(--ab-ink);
  outline-offset: -2px;
}

:is(.ab.sb-ab, .sb-gen) .ab-row.is-picked {
  background-color: var(--ab-pri-tint);
}

:is(.ab.sb-ab, .sb-gen) .sb-field .ab-input {
  margin-bottom: 16px;
}

:is(.ab.sb-ab, .sb-gen) .ab-toggle.sb-tog {
  min-height: 54px;
  padding: 6px 0px;
}

.sb-phone-in {
  position: relative;
  z-index: 1;
  height: 100%;
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  padding: 54px var(--k-margin, 20px) 0;
}

.sb-phone-in > * {
  flex-shrink: 0;
}

.sb-statusbar {
  position: absolute;
  top: 0px;
  left: 0px;
  right: 0px;
  height: 54px;
  padding: 4px 30px 0px 46px;
  display: flex;
  justify-content: space-between;
  align-items: center;
  font: 600 17px / 1 -apple-system, system-ui, sans-serif;
  letter-spacing: 0px;
  color: var(--ab-ink);
  text-transform: none;
}

.sb-sb-r {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 6px;
}

.sb-sig {
  width: 18px;
  height: 11px;
  background: linear-gradient(90deg, currentcolor 0px, currentcolor 3px, transparent 3px, transparent 5px, currentcolor 5px, currentcolor 8px, transparent 8px, transparent 10px, currentcolor 10px, currentcolor 13px, transparent 13px, transparent 15px, currentcolor 15px, currentcolor 18px);
  clip-path: polygon(0px 75%, 100% 0px, 100% 100%, 0px 100%);
}

.sb-wifi {
  width: 16px;
  height: 11px;
  border-radius: 14px 14px 0px 0px;
  background: currentcolor;
  clip-path: polygon(50% 100%, 0px 20%, 100% 20%);
}

.sb-bat {
  position: relative;
  width: 26px;
  height: 13px;
  border: 1.5px solid;
  border-radius: 4px;
  opacity: 0.9;
}

.sb-bat::after {
  content: "";
  position: absolute;
  inset: 1.5px 6px 1.5px 1.5px;
  border-radius: 1.5px;
  background: currentcolor;
}

.sb-homebar {
  position: absolute;
  z-index: 3;
  bottom: 8px;
  left: 50%;
  width: 134px;
  height: 5px;
  margin-left: -67px;
  border-radius: 3px;
  background: var(--ab-ink);
  opacity: 0.85;
}

:is(.ab.sb-ab, .sb-gen).sb-kit .ab-headline.sb-ob-h {
  max-width: none;
  text-wrap: balance;
  margin: 0px !important;
  font-size: var(--k-large-title, 34px) !important;
  line-height: var(--k-large-title-lh, 41px) !important;
}

:is(.ab.sb-ab, .sb-gen).sb-kit .ab-subcopy.sb-ob-body {
  max-width: none;
  margin: 12px 0px 0px !important;
  font-size: var(--k-body, 17px) !important;
  line-height: var(--k-body-lh, 24px) !important;
}

:is(.ab.sb-ab, .sb-gen) .sb-k-row.sb-k-cut {
  display: none !important;
}

.sb-tabs {
  position: relative;
  z-index: 2;
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: repeat(4, minmax(0px, 1fr));
  align-items: start;
  height: var(--k-tab, 83px);
  margin: auto calc(-1 * var(--k-margin, 20px)) 0;
  padding: 6px 6px 34px;
  border-top: 1px solid var(--ab-line);
  background: color-mix(in srgb, var(--ab-canvas) 92%, transparent);
}

.sb-tab {
  position: relative;
  display: grid;
  justify-items: center;
  gap: 3px;
  min-width: 0px;
  padding: 2px 0px 6px;
  border: 0px;
  background: none;
  cursor: pointer;
  font-family: var(--sb-ui-ff, var(--ab-font-ui));
  font-size: var(--k-tab-label, 10px);
  line-height: var(--k-tab-label-lh, 12px);
  font-weight: 500;
  color: color-mix(in srgb, var(--ab-ink) 28%, var(--ab-muted));
  text-transform: var(--sb-ui-tt, none);
  letter-spacing: var(--sb-ui-ls, 0);
}

.sb-tab span {
  max-width: 100%;
  white-space: nowrap;
}

.sb-tab[aria-current] {
  color: var(--ab-ink);
  font-weight: 600;
}

.sb-tab[aria-current]::before {
  content: "";
  position: absolute;
  bottom: 0px;
  left: 50%;
  width: 16px;
  margin-left: -8px;
  height: 3px;
  border-radius: 3px;
  background: var(--ab-primary);
}

.sb-tab[aria-current] > .sb-ico {
  color: var(--k-pri-text, var(--ab-primary-text, var(--ab-primary)));
}

.sb-tab:focus-visible {
  outline: 2px solid var(--ab-ink);
  outline-offset: -2px;
}

.sb-k-mid {
  margin: 0px 0.12em;
}

:is(.ab.sb-ab, .sb-gen).sb-kit :is(.ab-card, .ab-panel, .ab-list) {
  --k-muted: color-mix(in srgb, var(--ab-ink) 28%, var(--ab-muted));
  --k-picked: color-mix(in srgb, var(--ab-primary) 12%, transparent);
}

:is(.ab.sb-ab, .sb-gen).sb-kit .ab-row.sb-k-row.is-picked {
  background-color: var(--k-picked, var(--ab-pri-tint));
}

:is(.ab.sb-ab, .sb-gen).sb-kit {
  --k-ui: var(--sb-ui-ff, var(--ab-font-ui, var(--ab-font-b)));
  --k-muted: color-mix(in srgb, var(--ab-ink) 28%, var(--ab-muted));
  --k-r: min(var(--sb-r, 12px), 16px);
  --sb-focus-w: 2px;
  --sb-focus-offset: 2px;
}

:where(.ab.sb-ab, .sb-gen).sb-kit .sb-k-focus,
:where(.ab.sb-ab, .sb-gen).sb-kit :where(.sb-k-btn, .sb-k-btn2, .sb-k-textbtn, .sb-k-danger, .sb-k-iconbtn):focus-visible {
  outline: var(--sb-focus-w) solid var(--sb-focus, var(--sb-focus-auto, var(--ab-ink)));
  outline-offset: var(--sb-focus-offset);
}

:is(.ab.sb-ab, .sb-gen).sb-kit :is(.ab-cta.sb-k-btn, .ab-type-apply.sb-k-btn2) {
  width: 100%;
  height: auto;
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 10px;
  white-space: normal;
  min-height: var(--k-btn, 52px) !important;
  padding-top: 0px !important;
  padding-bottom: 0px !important;
  font-size: var(--k-headline, 17px) !important;
  line-height: var(--k-headline-lh, 22px) !important;
}

:is(.ab.sb-ab, .sb-gen).sb-kit .ab-type-apply.sb-k-btn2 {
  justify-content: center;
  text-align: center;
}

:is(.ab.sb-ab, .sb-gen).sb-kit .ab-cta.sb-k-btn .ab-cta-arrow {
  flex: 0 0 auto;
}

:is(.ab.sb-ab, .sb-gen).sb-kit .ab-type-apply.sb-k-btn2.sb-k-auto {
  width: auto;
  padding-left: 20px !important;
  padding-right: 20px !important;
}

:is(.ab.sb-ab, .sb-gen).sb-kit .sb-k-pressed {
  box-shadow: inset 0 0 0 100vmax color-mix(in srgb, var(--ab-ink) 7%, transparent);
}

:is(.ab.sb-ab, .sb-gen).sb-kit :is(.sb-k-btn, .sb-k-btn2, .sb-k-textbtn, .sb-k-danger, .sb-k-iconbtn):disabled {
  cursor: not-allowed;
  color: var(--k-dis-label, var(--k-muted)) !important;
  box-shadow: inset 0 0 0 100vmax color-mix(in srgb, var(--ab-canvas) 58%, transparent) !important;
}

:is(.ab.sb-ab, .sb-gen).sb-kit :is(.sb-k-btn, .sb-k-btn2):disabled * {
  color: inherit !important;
}

:is(.ab.sb-ab, .sb-gen).sb-kit :is(.sb-k-box, .ab-switch):disabled {
  opacity: 0.5;
  cursor: not-allowed;
}

:is(.ab.sb-ab, .sb-gen).sb-kit :is(.sb-k-check, .ab-toggle).is-disabled {
  cursor: not-allowed;
}

:is(.ab.sb-ab, .sb-gen).sb-kit :is(.sb-k-check, .ab-toggle).is-disabled > span {
  color: var(--k-muted) !important;
}

.sb-k-textbtn {
  min-height: var(--k-tap, 44px);
  padding: 0px 4px;
  border: 0px;
  background: none;
  cursor: pointer;
  font-family: var(--k-ui);
  font-size: var(--k-headline, 17px);
  line-height: var(--k-headline-lh, 22px);
  font-weight: 600;
  color: var(--k-pri-text, var(--ab-primary-text, var(--ab-ink)));
  text-transform: var(--sb-ui-tt, none);
  letter-spacing: var(--sb-ui-ls, 0);
  white-space: nowrap;
}

:is(.ab.sb-ab, .sb-gen).sb-kit .sb-k-textbtn.sb-k-pressed {
  box-shadow: none;
  color: var(--k-muted);
}

.sb-k-danger {
  width: 100%;
  min-height: var(--k-btn, 52px);
  padding: 0px 20px;
  cursor: pointer;
  border: 1px solid var(--sb-danger);
  border-radius: min(var(--sb-r, 10px), 999px);
  background: var(--sb-danger);
  color: var(--sb-danger-on, #fff);
  font-family: var(--k-ui);
  font-size: var(--k-headline, 17px);
  font-weight: 600;
  text-transform: var(--sb-ui-tt, none);
  letter-spacing: var(--sb-ui-ls, 0);
  box-shadow: 0 1px 2px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.04), 0 6px 16px -8px color-mix(in srgb, var(--sb-danger) 35%, transparent);
}

:is(.ab.sb-ab, .sb-gen)[data-sb-danger="ink"] .sb-k-danger {
  border: 1.5px solid var(--ab-ink);
  background: transparent;
  color: var(--ab-ink);
  box-shadow: none;
}

:is(.ab.sb-ab, .sb-gen)[data-sb-danger="ink"].sb-kit .sb-k-danger.sb-k-pressed {
  background: color-mix(in srgb, var(--ab-ink) 8%, transparent);
}

:is(.ab.sb-ab, .sb-gen).sb-kit .ab-type-apply.sb-k-iconbtn {
  box-sizing: border-box;
  width: var(--k-tap, 44px);
  height: var(--k-tap, 44px);
  min-width: var(--k-tap, 44px) !important;
  min-height: var(--k-tap, 44px) !important;
  padding: 0px !important;
}

.sb-k-plain {
  display: grid;
  place-items: center;
  width: var(--k-tap, 44px);
  height: var(--k-tap, 44px);
  padding: 0px;
  border: 0px;
  background: none;
  color: var(--ab-ink);
  cursor: pointer;
}

:is(.ab.sb-ab, .sb-gen).sb-kit .sb-k-split {
  display: flex;
  width: 100%;
}

:is(.ab.sb-ab, .sb-gen).sb-kit .sb-k-split > .sb-k-split-main {
  flex: 1 1 0px;
  width: auto;
  min-width: 0px;
  border-top-right-radius: 0px !important;
  border-bottom-right-radius: 0px !important;
}

:is(.ab.sb-ab, .sb-gen).sb-kit .sb-k-split > .sb-k-split-more {
  flex: 0 0 auto;
  display: grid;
  place-items: center;
  box-sizing: border-box;
  width: var(--k-btn, 52px);
  margin-left: -1px;
  border-left: 1px solid color-mix(in srgb, var(--ab-on-primary) 40%, transparent);
  cursor: pointer;
  min-height: var(--k-btn, 52px) !important;
  padding: 0px !important;
  border-top-left-radius: 0px !important;
  border-bottom-left-radius: 0px !important;
}

:is(.ab.sb-ab, .sb-gen).sb-kit .sb-k-splitwrap > .sb-k-menu {
  width: min(100%, 240px);
  margin: 6px 0px 0px auto !important;
}

.sb-k-fabbox {
  position: relative;
  width: 100%;
  padding-bottom: 76px;
  overflow: hidden;
  border: 1px solid var(--ab-line);
  border-radius: var(--k-r);
  background: var(--ab-canvas);
}

:is(.ab.sb-ab, .sb-gen).sb-kit .sb-k-fabbox > :is(.sb-k-fab, .sb-k-backtop) {
  position: absolute;
  right: 16px;
  bottom: 16px;
}

:is(.ab.sb-ab, .sb-gen).sb-kit .ab-cta.sb-k-fab {
  display: grid;
  place-items: center;
  width: 56px;
  height: 56px;
  cursor: pointer;
  min-width: 0px !important;
  min-height: 0px !important;
  padding: 0px !important;
}

:where(.ab.sb-ab, .sb-gen).sb-kit :is(.sb-k-fab, .sb-k-split-more):focus-visible {
  outline: var(--sb-focus-w, 2px) solid var(--sb-focus, var(--sb-focus-auto, var(--ab-ink)));
  outline-offset: var(--sb-focus-offset, 2px);
}

.sb-k-link {
  color: var(--k-pri-text, var(--ab-primary-text, var(--ab-ink)));
  text-decoration: underline;
  text-underline-offset: 3px;
  cursor: pointer;
}

:is(.ab.sb-ab, .sb-gen).sb-kit .sb-k-link.sb-k-pressed {
  box-shadow: none;
  color: var(--k-muted);
}

.sb-k-link:focus-visible {
  outline: var(--sb-focus-w, 2px) solid var(--sb-focus, var(--sb-focus-auto, var(--ab-ink)));
  outline-offset: 2px;
}

:is(.ab.sb-ab, .sb-gen).sb-kit .sb-k-field .ab-fieldlabel,
:is(.ab.sb-ab, .sb-gen).sb-kit .sb-k-selectwrap .ab-fieldlabel {
  margin: 0px 0px 8px !important;
  font-size: var(--k-footnote, 13px) !important;
  line-height: var(--k-footnote-lh, 18px) !important;
}

:is(.ab.sb-ab, .sb-gen).sb-kit :is(.sb-k-field, .sb-k-search, .sb-k-selectwrap) .ab-input {
  min-height: 0px;
  line-height: normal;
  height: var(--k-field, 48px) !important;
  margin: 0px !important;
  padding-top: 0px !important;
  padding-bottom: 0px !important;
  font-size: var(--k-body, 17px) !important;
}

:is(.ab.sb-ab, .sb-gen).sb-kit .sb-k-field.is-focus .ab-input,
:is(.ab.sb-ab, .sb-gen).sb-kit .sb-k-search.is-focus .ab-input {
  border-color: var(--k-pri-text, var(--ab-primary-text)) !important;
  box-shadow: 0 0 0 3px color-mix(in srgb, var(--ab-primary) 24%, transparent) !important;
}

:is(.ab.sb-ab, .sb-gen).sb-kit .sb-k-field.is-error .ab-input {
  border-color: var(--sb-danger) !important;
  box-shadow: 0 0 0 3px color-mix(in srgb, var(--sb-danger) 18%, transparent) !important;
}

[data-sb-danger="ink"][data-k-field="box"].sb-kit .sb-k-field.is-error .ab-input {
  border-width: 2px !important;
}

:is(.ab.sb-ab, .sb-gen).sb-kit .sb-k-field .ab-input:disabled {
  opacity: 0.5;
  cursor: not-allowed;
}

.sb-k-help {
  font-family: var(--ab-font-b);
  font-size: var(--k-footnote, 13px);
  line-height: var(--k-footnote-lh, 18px);
  color: var(--k-muted);
  margin: 6px 0px 0px !important;
}

.sb-k-help.is-error {
  color: var(--sb-danger-text, var(--sb-danger));
}

.sb-k-search {
  width: 100%;
}

:is(.ab.sb-ab, .sb-gen).sb-kit .sb-k-search .ab-input {
  padding-left: 44px !important;
}

.sb-k-search > .sb-ico {
  left: 14px;
}

.sb-k-selectwrap {
  width: 100%;
}

:is(.ab.sb-ab, .sb-gen).sb-kit .ab-input.sb-k-select {
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: space-between;
  gap: 10px;
  width: 100%;
  text-align: left;
  cursor: pointer;
  display: flex !important;
}

.sb-k-menu {
  list-style: none;
  padding: 6px;
  border: 1px solid var(--ab-line);
  border-radius: var(--k-r);
  background: var(--ab-canvas);
  color: var(--ab-ink);
  box-shadow: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.04) 0px 1px 2px, rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.16) 0px 10px 24px -10px;
  margin: 6px 0px 0px !important;
}

.sb-k-menu li {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: space-between;
  gap: 10px;
  min-height: var(--k-tap, 44px);
  padding: 0px 12px;
  border-radius: max(calc(var(--k-r) - 4px), 0px);
  font-family: var(--ab-font-ui);
  font-size: var(--k-body, 17px);
}

.sb-k-menu li[aria-selected="true"] {
  background: var(--ab-pri-tint);
  font-weight: 600;
}

.sb-k-menu .sb-ico {
  color: var(--k-pri-on-tint, var(--ab-primary-text));
}

.sb-k-check {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 12px;
  min-height: var(--k-tap, 44px);
  font-family: var(--ab-font-ui);
  font-size: var(--k-body, 17px);
  line-height: var(--k-body-lh, 24px);
  cursor: pointer;
}

.sb-k-box {
  appearance: none;
  position: relative;
  flex: 0 0 auto;
  display: grid;
  place-items: center;
  width: 24px;
  height: 24px;
  margin: 0px;
  cursor: pointer;
  border: 1.5px solid color-mix(in srgb, var(--ab-ink) 72%, transparent);
  border-radius: min(var(--sb-r, 6px), 7px);
  background: transparent;
}

.sb-k-box[type="radio"] {
  border-radius: 50%;
}

.sb-k-box:checked {
  background: var(--ab-primary);
  border-color: var(--ab-primary);
}

.sb-k-box[type="checkbox"]:checked::after {
  content: "";
  width: 12px;
  height: 6px;
  border-left: 2.5px solid var(--ab-on-primary);
  border-bottom: 2.5px solid var(--ab-on-primary);
  transform: translateY(-2px) rotate(-45deg);
}

.sb-k-box[type="radio"]:checked::after {
  content: "";
  width: 10px;
  height: 10px;
  border-radius: 50%;
  background: var(--ab-on-primary);
}

.sb-k-box:focus-visible {
  outline: var(--sb-focus-w, 2px) solid var(--sb-focus, var(--sb-focus-auto, var(--ab-ink)));
  outline-offset: var(--sb-focus-offset, 2px);
}

:is(.ab.sb-ab, .sb-gen).sb-kit .ab-toggle.sb-tog {
  width: 100%;
  line-height: var(--k-body-lh, 24px);
  font-size: var(--k-body, 17px) !important;
}

:is(.ab.sb-ab, .sb-gen).sb-kit .ab-seg {
  width: 100%;
}

:is(.ab.sb-ab, .sb-gen).sb-kit .ab-seg-btn {
  white-space: nowrap;
  min-height: var(--k-tap, 44px) !important;
  font-size: var(--k-subhead, 15px) !important;
  line-height: var(--k-subhead-lh, 20px) !important;
}

.sb-k-slider,
.sb-k-progress {
  width: 100%;
}

.sb-k-slider-h {
  display: flex;
  justify-content: space-between;
  gap: 12px;
  margin-bottom: 8px;
  font-family: var(--ab-font-ui);
  font-size: var(--k-subhead, 15px);
  line-height: var(--k-subhead-lh, 20px);
}

.sb-k-slider-h span:last-child {
  color: var(--k-muted);
  font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums;
}

:is(.ab.sb-ab, .sb-gen) input[type="range"].sb-k-range {
  appearance: none;
  display: block;
  width: 100%;
  height: 28px;
  margin: 0px;
  background: transparent;
  cursor: pointer;
}

:is(.ab.sb-ab, .sb-gen) input[type="range"].sb-k-range::-webkit-slider-runnable-track {
  height: 4px;
  border-radius: 2px;
  background: linear-gradient(90deg, var(--ab-primary) var(--v, 50%), color-mix(in srgb, var(--ab-ink) 16%, transparent) var(--v, 50%));
}

:is(.ab.sb-ab, .sb-gen) input[type="range"].sb-k-range::-webkit-slider-thumb {
  appearance: none;
  width: 28px;
  height: 28px;
  margin-top: -12px;
  border-radius: 50%;
  background: var(--ab-canvas);
  border: 1px solid color-mix(in srgb, var(--ab-ink) 14%, transparent);
  box-shadow: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.06) 0px 1px 2px, rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.16) 0px 3px 8px -2px;
  transform: none;
}

:is(.ab.sb-ab, .sb-gen) input[type="range"].sb-k-range:focus-visible {
  outline: 2px solid var(--ab-ink);
  outline-offset: 4px;
}

.sb-k-icons {
  display: flex;
  flex-wrap: wrap;
  align-items: flex-end;
  gap: 20px;
}

.sb-k-icon {
  display: grid;
  justify-items: center;
  gap: 6px;
  color: var(--ab-ink);
  font-family: var(--ab-font-ui);
  font-size: var(--k-caption, 12px);
  line-height: var(--k-caption-lh, 16px);
}

.sb-k-icon > span {
  color: var(--k-muted);
  white-space: nowrap;
}

.sb-k-combo {
  width: 100%;
}

.sb-k-match {
  background: none;
  color: inherit;
  font-weight: 700;
}

.sb-k-listbox {
  width: 100%;
  box-shadow: none;
  margin: 0px !important;
}

.sb-k-listbox li > span {
  flex: 1 1 0%;
  min-width: 0px;
}

.sb-k-listbox .sb-k-box {
  pointer-events: none;
}

.sb-k-stepwrap {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: space-between;
  gap: 16px;
  width: 100%;
  font-family: var(--ab-font-ui);
  font-size: var(--k-body, 17px);
  line-height: var(--k-body-lh, 24px);
}

.sb-k-stepper {
  display: inline-flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 4px;
}

.sb-k-step-val {
  min-width: 36px;
  text-align: center;
  font-size: var(--k-body, 17px);
  font-weight: 600;
  font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums;
}

.sb-k-knobwrap {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 16px;
}

.sb-k-knob {
  position: relative;
  flex: 0 0 auto;
  width: 72px;
  height: 72px;
}

.sb-k-knob-arc {
  position: absolute;
  inset: 0px;
  border-radius: 50%;
  background: conic-gradient(from -135deg, var(--ab-primary) calc(var(--v) * 270deg), color-mix(in srgb, var(--ab-ink) 16%, transparent) 0deg 270deg, transparent 0deg);
  mask: radial-gradient(farthest-side, transparent calc(100% - 6px), rgb(0, 0, 0) calc(100% - 5px));
}

.sb-k-knob-dial {
  position: absolute;
  inset: 10px;
  border-radius: 50%;
  background: var(--ab-canvas);
  border: 1px solid color-mix(in srgb, var(--ab-ink) 14%, transparent);
  box-shadow: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.06) 0px 1px 2px, rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.16) 0px 3px 8px -2px;
  transform: rotate(calc(-135deg + var(--v) * 270deg));
}

.sb-k-knob-ind {
  position: absolute;
  left: 50%;
  top: 5px;
  width: 3px;
  height: 10px;
  margin-left: -1.5px;
  border-radius: 2px;
  background: var(--ab-ink);
}

.sb-k-knob-txt {
  display: grid;
  gap: 2px;
  font-family: var(--ab-font-ui);
  font-size: var(--k-subhead, 15px);
  line-height: var(--k-subhead-lh, 20px);
}

.sb-k-knob-val {
  color: var(--k-muted);
  font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums;
}

.sb-k-datefield {
  position: relative;
}

:is(.ab.sb-ab, .sb-gen).sb-kit .sb-k-datefield .ab-input {
  padding-right: 46px !important;
  cursor: pointer;
}

.sb-k-date-ico {
  position: absolute;
  right: 14px;
  top: calc(var(--k-footnote-lh, 18px) + 8px + (var(--k-field, 48px) - 20px) / 2);
  display: grid;
  color: var(--k-muted);
  pointer-events: none;
}

.sb-k-cal {
  width: 100%;
  padding: 8px 8px 12px;
  border: 1px solid var(--ab-line);
  border-radius: var(--k-r);
  background: var(--ab-canvas);
  color: var(--ab-ink);
  font-family: var(--ab-font-ui);
}

.sb-k-cal-head {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: var(--k-tap, 44px) minmax(0, 1fr) var(--k-tap, 44px);
  align-items: center;
}

.sb-k-cal-t {
  text-align: center;
  font-size: var(--k-headline, 17px);
  line-height: var(--k-headline-lh, 22px);
  font-weight: 600;
}

.sb-k-cal-grid {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: repeat(7, minmax(0px, 1fr));
}

.sb-k-cal-dow {
  display: grid;
  place-items: center;
  height: 28px;
  font-size: var(--k-caption, 12px);
  line-height: var(--k-caption-lh, 16px);
  font-weight: 600;
  color: var(--k-muted);
  text-transform: var(--sb-ui-tt, none);
  letter-spacing: var(--sb-ui-ls, 0);
}

.sb-k-day {
  height: var(--k-tap, 44px);
  padding: 0px;
  border: 0px;
  border-radius: min(var(--sb-r, 12px), 22px);
  background: none;
  color: var(--ab-ink);
  font-style: inherit;
  font-variant-ligatures: inherit;
  font-variant-caps: inherit;
  font-variant-east-asian: inherit;
  font-variant-alternates: inherit;
  font-variant-position: inherit;
  font-variant-emoji: inherit;
  font-weight: inherit;
  font-stretch: inherit;
  line-height: inherit;
  font-family: inherit;
  font-optical-sizing: inherit;
  font-size-adjust: inherit;
  font-kerning: inherit;
  font-feature-settings: inherit;
  font-variation-settings: inherit;
  font-language-override: inherit;
  font-size: var(--k-subhead, 15px);
  font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums;
  cursor: pointer;
}

.sb-k-day.is-out {
  color: var(--k-muted);
}

.sb-k-day.is-today {
  box-shadow: inset 0 0 0 1.5px var(--ab-ink);
}

.sb-k-day.is-range {
  border-radius: 0px;
  background: var(--ab-pri-tint);
  box-shadow: none;
}

.sb-k-day.is-sel {
  background: var(--ab-primary);
  color: var(--ab-on-primary);
  font-weight: 600;
  box-shadow: none;
}

.sb-k-day.is-start {
  border-radius: min(var(--sb-r, 12px), 22px) 0 0 min(var(--sb-r, 12px), 22px);
}

.sb-k-day.is-end {
  border-radius: 0 min(var(--sb-r, 12px), 22px) min(var(--sb-r, 12px), 22px) 0;
}

.sb-k-day:focus-visible {
  outline: var(--sb-focus-w, 2px) solid var(--sb-focus, var(--sb-focus-auto, var(--ab-ink)));
  outline-offset: -2px;
}

.sb-k-wheel {
  --wr: 40px;
  position: relative;
  display: flex;
  width: 100%;
  max-width: 420px;
  height: calc(var(--wr) * 5);
  overflow: hidden;
  border-radius: var(--k-r);
  background: var(--ab-canvas);
}

.sb-k-wheel-col {
  position: relative;
  z-index: 1;
  flex: 1 1 0%;
  min-width: 0px;
}

.sb-k-wheel-item {
  display: grid;
  place-items: center;
  height: var(--wr);
  font-family: var(--ab-font-ui);
  font-size: var(--k-body, 17px);
  color: var(--k-muted);
  font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums;
}

.sb-k-wheel-item.is-d2 {
  font-size: var(--k-subhead, 15px);
}

.sb-k-wheel-item.is-sel {
  color: var(--ab-ink);
  font-weight: 600;
  font-size: var(--k-title-2, 22px);
}

.sb-k-wheel-band {
  position: absolute;
  left: 6px;
  right: 6px;
  z-index: 0;
  top: calc(var(--wr) * 2);
  height: var(--wr);
  border-radius: min(var(--k-r), 12px);
  background: color-mix(in srgb, var(--ab-ink) 8%, transparent);
  pointer-events: none;
}

:is(.ab.sb-ab, .sb-gen).sb-kit .ab-card.sb-k-card {
  display: block;
  width: 100%;
  margin: 0px;
  padding-left: var(--k-cardpad, 20px) !important;
  padding-right: var(--k-cardpad, 20px) !important;
  padding-bottom: var(--k-cardpad, 20px) !important;
}

:is(.ab.sb-ab, .sb-gen).sb-kit .sb-k-card .ab-seg {
  margin: 0px !important;
}

:is(.ab.sb-ab, .sb-gen).sb-kit .sb-k-card .ab-card-title {
  margin: 16px 0px 6px !important;
  font-size: var(--k-title-2, 22px) !important;
  line-height: var(--k-title-2-lh, 28px) !important;
}

:is(.ab.sb-ab, .sb-gen).sb-kit .sb-k-card .ab-card-body {
  margin: 0px 0px 20px !important;
  font-size: var(--k-subhead, 15px) !important;
  line-height: var(--k-subhead-lh, 20px) !important;
}

:is(.ab.sb-ab, .sb-gen).sb-kit .ab-labelrow.sb-k-section {
  align-items: center;
  min-height: 32px;
  margin: 0px 0px 8px !important;
}

:is(.ab.sb-ab, .sb-gen).sb-kit .sb-k-textbtn.sb-k-see {
  margin: -6px 0px;
  font-size: var(--k-subhead, 15px);
  line-height: var(--k-subhead-lh, 20px);
}

.sb-k-chev {
  display: inline-flex;
}

:is(.ab.sb-ab, .sb-gen).sb-kit .sb-k-section .ab-label {
  font-size: var(--k-footnote, 13px) !important;
  line-height: var(--k-footnote-lh, 18px) !important;
}

:is(.ab.sb-ab, .sb-gen).sb-kit .ab-list.sb-k-list {
  width: 100%;
  margin: 0px;
  padding: 0px;
  list-style: none;
  display: block !important;
}

:is(.ab.sb-ab, .sb-gen).sb-kit .ab-row.sb-k-row {
  align-items: center;
  display: grid !important;
  grid-template-columns: auto minmax(0px, 1fr) auto !important;
  grid-template-rows: auto !important;
  gap: 0px 14px !important;
  min-height: var(--k-row, 52px) !important;
  height: auto !important;
  padding: 8px 16px !important;
  aspect-ratio: auto !important;
  font-size: var(--k-body, 17px) !important;
  line-height: var(--k-body-lh, 24px) !important;
}

:is(.ab.sb-ab, .sb-gen).sb-kit .sb-k-row > * {
  grid-area: 1 !important;
  position: static !important;
}

:is(.ab.sb-ab, .sb-gen).sb-kit .ab-labelrow.sb-k-section.is-inset {
  padding-left: var(--k-inset, 16px) !important;
  padding-right: var(--k-inset, 16px) !important;
}

.sb-k-lead {
  display: grid;
  place-items: center;
}

.sb-k-ico {
  display: grid;
  place-items: center;
  width: 32px;
  height: 32px;
  border-radius: min(var(--sb-r, 8px), 10px);
  background: var(--ab-pri-tint);
  color: var(--k-pri-on-tint, var(--ab-primary-text));
}

.sb-k-text {
  display: grid;
  min-width: 0px;
}

.sb-k-title {
  min-width: 0px;
  font-weight: 500;
  overflow-wrap: normal;
}

.sb-k-sub {
  font-family: var(--ab-font-b);
  font-size: var(--k-footnote, 13px);
  line-height: var(--k-footnote-lh, 18px);
  color: var(--k-muted);
}

.sb-k-trail {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 4px;
  font-size: var(--k-subhead, 15px);
  color: var(--k-muted);
  font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums;
  white-space: nowrap;
}

.sb-k-callout {
  --tone: var(--k-info, var(--ab-primary));
  --tone-ico: var(--k-info-ico, var(--ab-primary-text));
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: auto minmax(0px, 1fr);
  gap: 12px;
  width: 100%;
  padding: 14px 16px;
  border: var(--k-callout-edge-w, 1px) solid var(--tone-ico);
  border-radius: var(--k-callout-r, min(var(--sb-card-r, var(--k-r)), 16px));
  background: color-mix(in srgb, var(--tone) var(--k-callout-fill, 12%), var(--ab-canvas));
  color: var(--ab-ink);
  box-shadow: var(--k-callout-shadow, none);
  font-family: var(--ab-font-b);
  font-size: var(--k-callout, 16px);
  line-height: var(--k-callout-lh, 22px);
}

.sb-k-callout[data-tone="success"] {
  --tone: var(--k-ok, var(--ab-secondary));
  --tone-ico: var(--k-ok-ico, var(--k-ok, var(--ab-ink)));
}

.sb-k-callout[data-tone="warning"] {
  --tone: var(--k-warn, var(--ab-accent));
  --tone-ico: var(--k-warn-ico, var(--k-warn, var(--ab-ink)));
}

.sb-k-callout[data-tone="error"] {
  --tone: var(--k-err, var(--sb-danger));
  --tone-ico: var(--k-err-ico, var(--sb-danger-text, var(--sb-danger)));
}

[data-sb-danger="ink"] .sb-k-callout[data-tone="error"] {
  border: 2px solid var(--ab-ink);
  background: var(--ab-canvas);
}

.sb-k-callout > .sb-ico {
  margin-top: 1px;
  color: var(--tone-ico);
}

.sb-k-callout b {
  display: block;
  font-family: var(--ab-font-ui);
  font-weight: 600;
}

.sb-k-sheetbox {
  position: relative;
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  justify-content: flex-end;
  width: 100%;
  min-height: 300px;
  border-radius: var(--k-r);
  overflow: hidden;
  background: color-mix(in srgb, var(--ab-ink) 6%, var(--ab-canvas));
}

.sb-k-sheetbox::before {
  content: "";
  position: absolute;
  inset: 0px;
  background: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.38);
}

.sb-k-sheet {
  position: relative;
  display: grid;
  gap: 10px;
  padding: 10px var(--k-margin, 20px) var(--k-margin, 20px);
  border-radius: var(--k-sheet-r, min(var(--sb-card-r, var(--k-r)), 24px)) var(--k-sheet-r, min(var(--sb-card-r, var(--k-r)), 24px)) 0 0;
  background: var(--ab-canvas);
  color: var(--ab-ink);
  box-shadow: var(--k-sheet-shadow, 0 -1px 2px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.04), 0 -8px 24px -8px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.14));
}

.sb-k-grab {
  justify-self: center;
  width: 36px;
  height: 5px;
  border-radius: 3px;
  background: var(--ab-line);
}

:is(.ab.sb-ab, .sb-gen).sb-kit .ab-card-title.sb-k-sheet-t {
  margin: 6px 0px 0px !important;
  font-size: var(--k-title-2, 22px) !important;
  line-height: var(--k-title-2-lh, 28px) !important;
}

.sb-k-sheet-b,
.sb-k-para {
  font-family: var(--ab-font-b);
  font-size: var(--k-subhead, 15px);
  line-height: var(--k-subhead-lh, 20px);
  color: var(--k-muted);
  margin: 0px !important;
}

.sb-k-sheet-a {
  display: grid;
  gap: 10px;
  margin-top: 8px;
}

.sb-k-divider {
  display: grid;
  gap: 12px;
  width: 100%;
}

:is(.ab.sb-ab, .sb-gen).sb-kit .sb-k-divider .ab-labelrow {
  margin: 0px !important;
}

.sb-k-ph {
  display: grid;
  place-items: center;
  width: 100%;
  aspect-ratio: 16 / 10;
  border-radius: min(var(--sb-card-r, var(--k-r)), 12px);
  background: color-mix(in srgb, var(--ab-ink) 8%, var(--ab-canvas));
  color: var(--k-muted);
}

.sb-k-acc > summary {
  list-style: none;
  cursor: pointer;
}

.sb-k-acc > summary::-webkit-details-marker {
  display: none;
}

.sb-k-acc[open] .sb-k-acc-chev {
  transform: rotate(180deg);
}

.sb-k-acc-sum:focus-visible {
  outline: 2px solid var(--ab-ink);
  outline-offset: -2px;
}

:is(.ab.sb-ab, .sb-gen).sb-kit .sb-k-acc-body {
  font-family: var(--ab-font-b);
  font-size: var(--k-subhead, 15px);
  line-height: var(--k-subhead-lh, 20px);
  color: var(--k-muted);
  margin: 0px !important;
  padding: 4px 16px 16px !important;
}

.sb-k-carousel,
.sb-k-matrix {
  width: 100%;
  min-width: 0px;
}

.sb-k-car-track,
.sb-k-mx-track {
  display: flex;
  gap: 12px;
  overflow-x: auto;
  scroll-snap-type: x mandatory;
  scrollbar-width: none;
}

.sb-k-car-track::-webkit-scrollbar,
.sb-k-mx-track::-webkit-scrollbar {
  display: none;
}

:is(.ab.sb-ab, .sb-gen).sb-kit .ab-card.sb-k-car-card {
  flex: 0 0 min(72%, 240px);
  width: auto;
  scroll-snap-align: start;
  padding-top: var(--k-cardpad, 20px) !important;
}

:is(.ab.sb-ab, .sb-gen).sb-kit .sb-k-car-card .ab-card-body {
  margin-bottom: 0px !important;
}

.sb-k-dots {
  display: flex;
  justify-content: center;
  gap: 8px;
  margin: 12px 0px 0px;
}

.sb-k-dots i {
  width: 8px;
  height: 8px;
  border-radius: 50%;
  background: color-mix(in srgb, var(--ab-ink) 30%, var(--ab-canvas));
}

.sb-k-dots i.is-on {
  background: var(--k-pri-text, var(--ab-primary-text));
}

.sb-k-matrix {
  display: grid;
  gap: 8px;
}

.sb-k-mx-row {
  min-width: 0px;
}

.sb-k-mx-tile {
  flex: 0 0 112px;
  display: grid;
  align-content: start;
  gap: 8px;
  scroll-snap-align: start;
  font-family: var(--ab-font-ui);
  font-size: var(--k-subhead, 15px);
  line-height: var(--k-subhead-lh, 20px);
}

.sb-k-mx-tile .sb-k-ph {
  aspect-ratio: 1 / 1;
}

.sb-k-under {
  position: absolute;
  inset: 0px;
  z-index: 0;
  overflow: hidden;
  padding: 12px;
  background: var(--ab-canvas);
}

.sb-k-overlaybox {
  position: relative;
  display: grid;
  place-items: center;
  width: 100%;
  min-height: 240px;
  overflow: hidden;
  border-radius: var(--k-r);
  background: var(--ab-canvas);
}

.sb-k-scrim {
  position: absolute;
  inset: 0px;
  z-index: 1;
  background: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.38);
}

.sb-k-overlaybox > .sb-k-scrimnote {
  position: relative;
  z-index: 2;
  width: auto;
  padding: 0px 16px;
}

.sb-k-sheetbox.is-under::before {
  z-index: 1;
}

.sb-k-sheetbox.is-under > :not(.sb-k-under) {
  position: relative;
  z-index: 2;
}

.sb-k-dialogbox {
  justify-content: center;
  align-items: center;
  padding: 20px;
}

.sb-k-sidebox,
.sb-k-drawerbox {
  flex-direction: row;
  align-items: stretch;
  min-height: 380px;
}

.sb-k-sidebox {
  justify-content: flex-end;
}

.sb-k-drawerbox {
  justify-content: flex-start;
}

:is(.ab.sb-ab, .sb-gen).sb-kit .ab-card.sb-k-dialog {
  width: 100%;
  max-width: 340px;
  padding-top: var(--k-cardpad, 20px) !important;
}

:is(.ab.sb-ab, .sb-gen).sb-kit .sb-k-dialog .ab-card-title.sb-k-dialog-t {
  margin: 0px 0px 6px !important;
}

.sb-k-dialog-a {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: repeat(2, minmax(0px, 1fr));
  gap: 10px;
}

.sb-k-side,
.sb-k-drawer {
  display: grid;
  align-content: start;
  gap: 8px;
  width: min(80%, 300px);
  padding: 8px 16px 16px;
  background: var(--ab-canvas);
  color: var(--ab-ink);
}

.sb-k-side {
  border-left: 1px solid var(--ab-line);
  border-radius: var(--k-sheet-r, min(var(--sb-card-r, var(--k-r)), 24px)) 0 0 var(--k-sheet-r, min(var(--sb-card-r, var(--k-r)), 24px));
  box-shadow: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.14) -8px 0px 24px -8px;
}

.sb-k-drawer {
  padding: 12px 8px;
  border-right: 1px solid var(--ab-line);
  border-radius: 0 var(--k-sheet-r, min(var(--sb-card-r, var(--k-r)), 24px)) var(--k-sheet-r, min(var(--sb-card-r, var(--k-r)), 24px)) 0;
  box-shadow: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.14) 8px 0px 24px -8px;
}

.sb-k-drawer .sb-k-tb-brand {
  justify-self: start;
  padding: 4px 12px 8px;
}

.sb-k-side-h {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: space-between;
  gap: 8px;
  margin-right: -12px;
}

.sb-k-popbox {
  position: relative;
  display: grid;
  align-content: start;
  justify-items: start;
  gap: 12px;
  width: 100%;
  padding: 16px;
  border-radius: var(--k-r);
  background: color-mix(in srgb, var(--ab-ink) 4%, var(--ab-canvas));
}

.sb-k-popbox.is-end {
  justify-items: end;
}

.sb-k-popbox.is-tooltip {
  justify-items: center;
  gap: 0px;
}

:is(.ab.sb-ab, .sb-gen).sb-kit .ab-card.sb-k-pop {
  position: relative;
  width: min(100%, 280px);
  padding-top: var(--k-cardpad, 20px) !important;
}

:is(.ab.sb-ab, .sb-gen).sb-kit .sb-k-pop .ab-card-title {
  margin: 0px 0px 6px !important;
  font-size: var(--k-headline, 17px) !important;
  line-height: var(--k-headline-lh, 22px) !important;
}

:is(.ab.sb-ab, .sb-gen).sb-kit .sb-k-pop .ab-card-body {
  margin: 0px 0px 8px !important;
}

.sb-k-pop-arrow {
  z-index: 2;
  box-sizing: border-box;
  top: -11px;
  left: 24px;
  width: 14px;
  height: 14px;
  min-width: 14px;
  min-height: 14px;
  margin: 0px;
  padding: 0px;
  border-style: inherit;
  border-color: inherit;
  border-width: var(--k-arrow-edge, 1px) 0 0 var(--k-arrow-edge, 1px);
  background: inherit;
  transform: rotate(45deg);
  position: absolute !important;
}

:is(.ab.sb-ab, .sb-gen).sb-kit :is(.sb-k-pop, .sb-k-tip, .sb-k-tooltip) {
  overflow: visible !important;
}

.sb-k-popbox.is-end .sb-k-pop-arrow {
  left: auto;
  right: 16px;
}

.sb-k-popbox > .sb-k-tooltip {
  position: relative;
  order: -1;
  margin-bottom: 10px;
  padding: 6px 10px;
  border-radius: min(var(--sb-r, 8px), 10px);
  background: var(--ab-ink);
  color: var(--ab-canvas);
  font-family: var(--ab-font-ui);
  font-size: var(--k-footnote, 13px);
  line-height: var(--k-footnote-lh, 18px);
  white-space: nowrap;
}

.sb-k-pop-arrow.is-down {
  top: auto;
  bottom: -11px;
  left: 50%;
  margin-left: -7px;
  border-width: 0 var(--k-arrow-edge, 1px) var(--k-arrow-edge, 1px) 0;
}

.sb-k-lightbox {
  position: relative;
  display: grid;
  place-items: center;
  width: 100%;
  min-height: 340px;
  padding: 56px 56px 48px;
  border-radius: var(--k-r);
  background: color-mix(in srgb, var(--ab-ink) 92%, var(--ab-canvas));
  color: var(--ab-canvas);
}

.sb-k-lb-img {
  max-width: 420px;
  background: color-mix(in srgb, var(--ab-canvas) 14%, var(--ab-ink));
  color: var(--ab-canvas);
}

.sb-k-lightbox .sb-k-plain {
  position: absolute;
  color: var(--ab-canvas);
}

.sb-k-lb-close {
  top: 4px;
  right: 4px;
}

.sb-k-lb-prev {
  left: 4px;
  top: 50%;
  margin-top: calc(var(--k-tap, 44px) / -2);
}

.sb-k-lb-next {
  right: 4px;
  top: 50%;
  margin-top: calc(var(--k-tap, 44px) / -2);
}

.sb-k-lb-cap {
  position: absolute;
  left: 0px;
  right: 0px;
  bottom: 14px;
  text-align: center;
  font-family: var(--ab-font-ui);
  font-size: var(--k-footnote, 13px);
  line-height: var(--k-footnote-lh, 18px);
  margin: 0px !important;
}

.sb-k-topbar {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: var(--k-tap, 44px) minmax(0, 1fr) var(--k-tap, 44px);
  align-items: center;
  width: 100%;
  min-height: 44px;
  border-bottom: 1px solid var(--ab-line);
}

.sb-k-tb-title {
  text-align: center;
  font-family: var(--ab-font-ui);
  font-size: var(--k-headline, 17px);
  line-height: var(--k-headline-lh, 22px);
  font-weight: 600;
}

.sb-k-tabbarbox {
  width: 100%;
  overflow: hidden;
  border-radius: var(--k-r);
  border: 1px solid var(--ab-line);
}

.sb-k-tabbarbox .sb-tabs {
  margin: 0px;
  height: auto;
  padding-bottom: 14px;
  border-top: 0px;
}

.sb-k-tabs {
  display: flex;
  flex-wrap: wrap;
  gap: 0px 24px;
  width: 100%;
  border-bottom: 1px solid var(--ab-line);
}

.sb-k-tabs [role="tab"] {
  position: relative;
  min-height: var(--k-tap, 44px);
  padding: 0px;
  border: 0px;
  background: none;
  cursor: pointer;
  font-family: var(--k-ui);
  font-size: var(--k-subhead, 15px);
  color: var(--k-muted);
  text-transform: var(--sb-ui-tt, none);
  letter-spacing: var(--sb-ui-ls, 0);
  white-space: nowrap;
}

.sb-k-tabs [role="tab"][aria-selected="true"] {
  color: var(--ab-ink);
  font-weight: 600;
}

.sb-k-tabs [role="tab"][aria-selected="true"]::after {
  content: "";
  position: absolute;
  left: 0px;
  right: 0px;
  bottom: -1px;
  height: 2px;
  background: var(--ab-primary);
}

.sb-k-tabs [role="tab"]:focus-visible {
  outline: 2px solid var(--ab-ink);
  outline-offset: 2px;
}

.sb-k-nm-item {
  color: inherit;
  text-decoration: none;
  cursor: pointer;
}

:is(.ab.sb-ab, .sb-gen).sb-kit .sb-k-nm-children :is(.ab-row.sb-k-row, .ab-row.sb-k-row.is-picked) {
  padding-left: 62px !important;
}

.sb-k-scroll {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: minmax(0px, 1fr) 12px;
  gap: 8px;
  width: 100%;
  height: 168px;
}

.sb-k-scrollbox {
  min-width: 0px;
  overflow: hidden;
}

.sb-k-sbar {
  position: relative;
  border-radius: 999px;
  background: color-mix(in srgb, var(--ab-ink) 8%, transparent);
}

.sb-k-sbar-thumb {
  position: absolute;
  left: 2px;
  right: 2px;
  top: var(--t);
  height: var(--h);
  border-radius: 999px;
  background: color-mix(in srgb, var(--ab-ink) 45%, transparent);
}

.sb-k-cmdmenu {
  width: min(100%, 240px);
}

.sb-k-cmdmenu li {
  gap: 10px;
}

.sb-k-cmdmenu li > span:not(.sb-k-kbd) {
  flex: 1 1 0%;
  min-width: 0px;
}

.sb-k-cmdmenu .sb-ico {
  flex: 0 0 auto;
  color: var(--k-muted);
}

.sb-k-cmdmenu li[aria-selected="true"] .sb-ico {
  color: var(--k-pri-on-tint, var(--ab-primary-text));
}

.sb-k-cmdmenu li.is-danger,
.sb-k-cmdmenu li.is-danger .sb-ico {
  color: var(--sb-danger-text, var(--sb-danger));
}

.sb-k-cmdmenu li.sb-k-menu-sep {
  min-height: 0px;
  height: 1px;
  margin: 4px 8px;
  padding: 0px;
  background: var(--ab-line);
}

.sb-k-ddwrap {
  width: 100%;
}

.sb-k-ctxbox {
  display: grid;
  gap: 12px;
  width: 100%;
  padding: 12px;
  border-radius: var(--k-r);
  background: color-mix(in srgb, var(--ab-ink) 4%, var(--ab-canvas));
}

.sb-k-ctxbox > .sb-k-cmdmenu.sb-k-ctx {
  margin: 0px 0px 0px 56px !important;
}

.sb-k-subwrap {
  display: flex;
  flex-wrap: wrap;
  gap: 8px;
  align-items: flex-start;
  width: 100%;
}

.sb-k-subwrap > .sb-k-cmdmenu {
  width: min(100%, 220px);
  margin: 0px !important;
}

.sb-k-subwrap > .sb-k-cmdmenu + .sb-k-cmdmenu {
  margin-top: var(--k-tap, 44px) !important;
}

.sb-k-piebox {
  display: grid;
  place-items: center;
  width: 100%;
}

.sb-k-pie {
  --pr: 76px;
  position: relative;
  width: 100%;
  max-width: 224px;
  aspect-ratio: 1 / 1;
}

.sb-k-pie::before {
  content: "";
  position: absolute;
  inset: calc(50% - var(--pr));
  border: 1px solid var(--ab-line);
  border-radius: 50%;
  background: color-mix(in srgb, var(--ab-ink) 3%, transparent);
}

.sb-k-pie-hub {
  position: absolute;
  left: 50%;
  top: 50%;
  width: 14px;
  height: 14px;
  margin: -7px 0px 0px -7px;
  border-radius: 50%;
  background: var(--ab-primary);
}

:is(.ab.sb-ab, .sb-gen).sb-kit .sb-k-pie > .sb-k-pie-item {
  position: absolute;
  border-radius: 50%;
  transform: translate(-50%, -50%);
}

.sb-k-row-inline {
  display: flex;
  flex-wrap: wrap;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 10px;
}

.sb-k-badge,
.sb-k-count {
  display: inline-flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: center;
  min-height: 22px;
  min-width: 22px;
  padding: 0px 8px;
  border-radius: 999px;
  font-family: var(--k-ui);
  font-size: var(--k-caption, 12px);
  line-height: var(--k-caption-lh, 16px);
  font-weight: 600;
  font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums;
}

.sb-k-badge,
.sb-k-count.is-accent {
  background: var(--ab-accent);
  color: var(--ab-on-accent);
}

.sb-k-count {
  background: color-mix(in srgb, var(--ab-ink) 10%, transparent);
  color: var(--ab-ink);
}

.sb-k-dotwrap {
  position: relative;
  display: grid;
  place-items: center;
  width: var(--k-tap, 44px);
  height: var(--k-tap, 44px);
  color: var(--ab-ink);
}

.sb-k-dotwrap .sb-badge {
  top: 9px;
  right: 10px;
}

:is(.ab.sb-ab, .sb-gen).sb-kit .ab-row-bar.sb-k-bar {
  width: 100%;
  display: block !important;
  height: 8px !important;
}

.sb-k-toast {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 12px;
  width: 100%;
  min-height: var(--k-btn, 52px);
  padding: 0px 6px 0px 16px;
  border-radius: min(var(--sb-r, 12px), 14px);
  background: var(--ab-ink);
  color: var(--ab-canvas);
  box-shadow: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.05) 0px 1px 2px, rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.2) 0px 8px 20px -8px;
  font-family: var(--ab-font-ui);
  font-size: var(--k-subhead, 15px);
  line-height: var(--k-subhead-lh, 20px);
}

.sb-k-toast > span {
  flex: 1 1 0%;
  min-width: 0px;
}

.sb-k-toast button {
  min-height: var(--k-tap, 44px);
  padding: 0px 12px;
  border: 0px;
  background: none;
  color: inherit;
  font: 600 var(--k-subhead, 15px)/1 var(--k-ui);
  cursor: pointer;
  text-decoration: underline;
  text-underline-offset: 3px;
}

.sb-k-empty {
  display: grid;
  justify-items: center;
  gap: 10px;
  width: 100%;
  padding: 20px 12px;
  text-align: center;
}

.sb-k-emptyico {
  display: grid;
  place-items: center;
  width: 56px;
  height: 56px;
  border-radius: 50%;
  background: var(--ab-pri-tint);
  color: var(--k-pri-on-tint, var(--ab-primary-text));
}

:is(.ab.sb-ab, .sb-gen).sb-kit .ab-card-title.sb-k-empty-t {
  margin: 4px 0px 0px !important;
  font-size: var(--k-title-2, 22px) !important;
  line-height: var(--k-title-2-lh, 28px) !important;
}

.sb-k-empty-b {
  max-width: 28ch;
  font-family: var(--ab-font-b);
  font-size: var(--k-subhead, 15px);
  line-height: var(--k-subhead-lh, 20px);
  color: var(--k-muted);
  margin: 0px 0px 6px !important;
}

.sb-k-av {
  flex: 0 0 auto;
  display: grid;
  place-items: center;
  width: 40px;
  height: 40px;
  border-radius: 50%;
  background: var(--ab-secondary);
  color: var(--ab-on-secondary);
  font: 600 15px/1 var(--k-ui);
  letter-spacing: 0px;
  text-transform: uppercase;
}

.sb-k-av-s {
  width: 32px;
  height: 32px;
  font-size: 13px;
}

.sb-k-av-l {
  width: 56px;
  height: 56px;
  font-size: 20px;
}

.sb-k-avs {
  display: inline-flex;
  padding-left: 4px;
}

.sb-k-avs .sb-k-av {
  margin-left: -4px;
  box-shadow: 0 0 0 2px var(--ab-canvas) !important;
}

.sb-k-spinner {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 12px;
  color: var(--k-muted);
  font-family: var(--ab-font-ui);
  font-size: var(--k-subhead, 15px);
  line-height: var(--k-subhead-lh, 20px);
}

.sb-k-spin {
  flex: 0 0 auto;
  width: 28px;
  height: 28px;
  border-top-style:;
  border-top-width:;
  border-right-color:;
  border-right-style:;
  border-right-width:;
  border-bottom-color:;
  border-bottom-style:;
  border-bottom-width:;
  border-left-color:;
  border-left-style:;
  border-left-width:;
  border-image-source:;
  border-image-slice:;
  border-image-width:;
  border-image-outset:;
  border-image-repeat:;
  border-top-color: var(--ab-primary);
  border-radius: 50%;
}

.sb-k-skel {
  display: grid;
  gap: 14px;
  width: 100%;
}

.sb-k-skel-row {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: auto minmax(0px, 1fr);
  gap: 14px;
  align-items: center;
}

.sb-k-skel-row > span {
  display: grid;
  gap: 8px;
}

.sb-k-skel-av,
.sb-k-skel-line,
.sb-k-skel-block {
  position: relative;
  display: block;
  overflow: hidden;
  border-radius: min(var(--sb-r, 6px), 8px);
  background-color: color-mix(in srgb, var(--ab-ink) 8%, var(--ab-canvas));
}

.sb-k-skel-av {
  width: 40px;
  height: 40px;
  border-radius: 50%;
}

.sb-k-skel-line {
  height: 12px;
}

.sb-k-skel-line.is-l1 {
  width: 62%;
  height: 14px;
}

.sb-k-skel-line.is-l2 {
  width: 38%;
}

.sb-k-skel-block {
  height: 96px;
  border-radius: min(var(--sb-card-r, var(--k-r)), 12px);
}

@media (prefers-reduced-motion: no-preference) {
  .sb-k-spin {
    animation: 0.9s linear 0s infinite normal none running sb-k-spin;
  }

  .sb-k-skel-av::after,
  .sb-k-skel-line::after,
  .sb-k-skel-block::after {
    content: "";
    position: absolute;
    inset: 0px;
    background: linear-gradient(90deg, transparent 33%, color-mix(in srgb, var(--ab-canvas) 55%, transparent) 50%, transparent 67%) 0 0 / 300% 100% no-repeat;
    animation: 1.4s ease-in-out 0s infinite normal none running sb-k-shimmer;
  }
}

@keyframes sb-k-spin { 
  100% { transform: rotate(360deg); }
}

@keyframes sb-k-shimmer { 
  0% { background-position: 100% 0px; }
  100% { background-position: 0px 0px; }
}

.sb-k-spec {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  min-width: 0px;
}

.sb-k-scr {
  position: relative;
  width: 390px;
  height: 844px;
  border-radius: 54px;
  overflow: hidden;
  container: ab / inline-size;
  background: var(--ab-canvas);
  color: var(--ab-ink);
  text-align: left;
}

.sb-k-scr-in {
  padding-bottom: 34px;
}

.sb-k-scr[data-scr="list"] .sb-k-scr-in {
  padding-bottom: 0px;
}

.sb-k-blk {
  flex-shrink: 0;
  min-width: 0px;
}

.sb-k-blk + .sb-k-blk {
  margin-top: 16px;
}

.sb-k-blk[data-blk="topbar"] + .sb-k-blk {
  margin-top: 12px;
}

.sb-k-blk[data-blk="section"] {
  margin-top: var(--k-gap, 28px);
}

.sb-k-blk[data-blk="section"] + .sb-k-blk {
  margin-top: 0px;
}

.sb-k-blk[data-blk="title"] + .sb-k-blk[data-blk="body"] {
  margin-top: 12px;
}

.sb-k-blk[data-blk="btn-primary"] + .sb-k-blk[data-blk="btn-text"] {
  margin-top: 8px;
  display: grid;
  justify-items: center;
}

.sb-k-blk[data-blk="toggle"] {
  margin-top: 0px;
  padding: 4px 0px;
  border-bottom: 1px solid var(--ab-line);
}

.sb-k-scr[data-scr="form"] .sb-k-blk[data-blk="toggle"] {
  margin-top: 16px;
}

.sb-k-scr[data-scr="onboarding"] .sb-k-blk[data-blk="title"] {
  margin-top: auto;
}

.sb-k-scr[data-scr="onboarding"] .sb-k-blk[data-blk="list"] {
  margin-top: var(--k-gap, 28px);
  margin-bottom: auto;
}

.sb-k-scr[data-scr="onboarding"] .sb-k-blk[data-blk="btn-primary"],
.sb-k-scr[data-scr="form"] .sb-k-blk[data-blk="btn-primary"] {
  margin-top: auto;
  padding-top: 16px;
}

.sb-k-scr[data-scr="settings"] .sb-k-blk[data-blk="btn-destructive"] {
  margin-top: var(--k-gap, 28px);
}

.sb-k-blk.sb-k-fill {
  flex: 1 1 auto;
  min-height: 0px;
  overflow: hidden;
  padding-bottom: 12px;
}

.sb-k-blk.sb-k-fill + .sb-k-blk {
  margin-top: 0px;
}

.sb-k-blk[data-blk="tabbar"] {
  margin: auto calc(-1 * var(--k-margin, 20px)) 0 !important;
}

.sb-k-blk[data-blk="tabbar"] .sb-tabs {
  margin: 0px;
}

:is(.ab.sb-ab, .sb-gen).sb-kit .ab-card-title.sb-k-ltitle {
  overflow-wrap: normal;
  text-wrap: balance;
  margin: 0px !important;
  font-size: var(--k-large-title, 34px) !important;
  line-height: var(--k-large-title-lh, 41px) !important;
}

:is(.ab.sb-ab, .sb-gen).sb-kit .sb-k-scr .ab-card.sb-k-card .ab-card-title {
  margin-top: 0px !important;
  font-size: var(--k-title, 28px) !important;
  line-height: var(--k-title-lh, 34px) !important;
}

:is(.ab.sb-ab, .sb-gen).sb-kit .sb-k-scr .ab-card.sb-k-card {
  padding-top: var(--k-cardpad, 20px) !important;
}

:is(.ab.sb-ab, .sb-gen).sb-kit .sb-k-scr .sb-k-card .ab-card-body {
  margin-bottom: 0px !important;
}

:is(.ab.sb-ab, .sb-gen).sb-kit .sb-k-tb-brand {
  display: inline-flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: center;
  gap: 8px;
  min-width: 0px;
  max-width: 100%;
  line-height: var(--k-headline-lh, 22px);
  font-size: var(--k-headline, 17px) !important;
}

:is(.ab.sb-ab, .sb-gen).sb-kit .sb-k-tb-brand .ab-mark {
  width: 28px;
  height: 28px;
}

:is(.ab.sb-ab, .sb-gen).sb-kit .ab-row.sb-k-row.is-nolead {
  grid-template-columns: minmax(0px, 1fr) auto !important;
}

@container ab (max-width: 760px) {
  .sb-search {
    flex: 1 1 140px;
    width: auto;
  }
}

:is(.ab.sb-ab, .sb-gen).sb-kit .ab-subcopy.sb-ob-body {
  display: block !important;
}

:is(.ab.sb-ab, .sb-gen).sb-kit :is(.sb-ob-h, .sb-k-ltitle, .sb-home-h) .ab-hl {
  -webkit-box-decoration-break: slice !important;
  box-decoration-break: slice !important;
  padding-left: 0px !important;
  padding-right: 0px !important;
}

/* ===== inline <style> ===== */
.ab[data-ae="scent-box-clean"] .ab-cta,
.ab[data-ae="scent-box-clean"].sb-ab.sb-kit .ab-cta,
.ab[data-ae="scent-box-clean"] .ab-cta.sb-k-btn,
.ab[data-ae="scent-box-clean"].sb-ab.sb-kit .ab-cta.sb-k-btn,
.ab[data-ae="scent-box-clean"] .ab-type-apply,
.ab[data-ae="scent-box-clean"].sb-ab.sb-kit .ab-type-apply,

.ab[data-ae="scent-box-clean"].sb-ab.sb-kit .ab-type-apply.sb-k-btn2,
.ab[data-ae="scent-box-clean"] .sb-k-textbtn,
.ab[data-ae="scent-box-clean"].sb-ab.sb-kit .sb-k-textbtn,
.ab[data-ae="scent-box-clean"] .sb-k-danger,
.ab[data-ae="scent-box-clean"].sb-ab.sb-kit .sb-k-danger,
.ab[data-ae="scent-box-clean"] .ab-input,
.ab[data-ae="scent-box-clean"].sb-ab.sb-kit .ab-input,
.ab[data-ae="scent-box-clean"] .sb-k-field .ab-input,
.ab[data-ae="scent-box-clean"].sb-ab.sb-kit .sb-k-field .ab-input,
.ab[data-ae="scent-box-clean"] .sb-k-search .ab-input,
.ab[data-ae="scent-box-clean"].sb-ab.sb-kit .sb-k-search .ab-input,
.ab[data-ae="scent-box-clean"] .sb-k-select,
.ab[data-ae="scent-box-clean"].sb-ab.sb-kit .sb-k-select,
.ab[data-ae="scent-box-clean"] .ab-seg-btn,
.ab[data-ae="scent-box-clean"].sb-ab.sb-kit .ab-seg-btn {
  border-radius: 999px;
}

.ab[data-ae="scent-box-clean"] .ab-card,
.ab[data-ae="scent-box-clean"].sb-ab.sb-kit .ab-card,
.ab[data-ae="scent-box-clean"] .ab-card.sb-k-card,
.ab[data-ae="scent-box-clean"].sb-ab.sb-kit .ab-card.sb-k-card,
.ab[data-ae="scent-box-clean"] .ab-card.sb-k-dialog,
.ab[data-ae="scent-box-clean"].sb-ab.sb-kit .ab-card.sb-k-dialog,
.ab[data-ae="scent-box-clean"] .sb-k-dialog,
.ab[data-ae="scent-box-clean"].sb-ab.sb-kit .sb-k-dialog,
.ab[data-ae="scent-box-clean"] .sb-k-sheet,
.ab[data-ae="scent-box-clean"].sb-ab.sb-kit .sb-k-sheet,
.ab[data-ae="scent-box-clean"] .sb-k-toast,
.ab[data-ae="scent-box-clean"].sb-ab.sb-kit .sb-k-toast,
.ab[data-ae="scent-box-clean"] .sb-k-menu,
.ab[data-ae="scent-box-clean"].sb-ab.sb-kit .sb-k-menu,
.ab[data-ae="scent-box-clean"] .sb-k-callout,
.ab[data-ae="scent-box-clean"].sb-ab.sb-kit .sb-k-callout {
  border-radius: 18px;
}

.ab[data-ae="scent-box-clean"] .sb-k-badge,
.ab[data-ae="scent-box-clean"].sb-ab.sb-kit .sb-k-badge,
.ab[data-ae="scent-box-clean"] .sb-k-count,
.ab[data-ae="scent-box-clean"].sb-ab.sb-kit .sb-k-count,
.ab[data-ae="scent-box-clean"] .sb-k-tooltip,
.ab[data-ae="scent-box-clean"].sb-ab.sb-kit .sb-k-tooltip,
.ab[data-ae="scent-box-clean"] .sb-k-tabs [role="tab"],
.ab[data-ae="scent-box-clean"].sb-ab.sb-kit .sb-k-tabs [role="tab"] {
  border-radius: 9px;
}

.ab[data-ae="scent-box-clean"] .ab-switch,
.ab[data-ae="scent-box-clean"].sb-ab.sb-kit .ab-switch,
.ab[data-ae="scent-box-clean"] .ab-row-bar,
.ab[data-ae="scent-box-clean"].sb-ab.sb-kit .ab-row-bar,
.ab[data-ae="scent-box-clean"] .sb-k-bar,
.ab[data-ae="scent-box-clean"].sb-ab.sb-kit .sb-k-bar,
.ab[data-ae="scent-box-clean"] .sb-k-av,
.ab[data-ae="scent-box-clean"].sb-ab.sb-kit .sb-k-av {
  border-radius: 999px;
}

.ab[data-ae="scent-box-clean"] .ab-rule {
  height: 0px;
  border-right: 0px;
  border-bottom: 0px;
  border-left: 0px;
  border-image: none;
  border-top: 1px solid var(--ab-line);
  background: none;
}

.ab[data-ae="scent-box-clean"] :is(.sb-k-divider, .sb-k-menu-sep) {
  border-color: var(--ab-line);
}

.ab[data-ae="scent-box-clean"] .ab-row,
.ab[data-ae="scent-box-clean"].sb-ab.sb-kit .ab-row,
.ab[data-ae="scent-box-clean"] .sb-k-row,
.ab[data-ae="scent-box-clean"].sb-ab.sb-kit .sb-k-row {
  border-bottom: 1px solid var(--ab-line);
}

.ab[data-ae="scent-box-clean"] .ab-headline {
  font-weight: 500;
  word-spacing: 0em;
}

.ab[data-ae="scent-box-clean"] .ab-brand {
  font-weight: 500;
}

.ab[data-ae="scent-box-clean"] .ab-card-title {
  font-weight: 500;
}

.ab[data-ae="scent-box-clean"] :is(.ab-headline, .ab-hero h1, .ab-hero h2) {
  letter-spacing: -0.015em;
  line-height: 1.1;
}

.ab[data-ae="scent-box-clean"].sb-kit :is(button, input, a, [tabindex]):focus-visible,
.ab[data-ae="scent-box-clean"] :is(.ab-switch, .ab-cta, .ab-seg-btn, .ab-type-apply):focus-visible {
  outline: 2px solid var(--ab-ink);
  outline-offset: 2px;
}

.ab[data-ae="scent-box-clean"] .ab-cta,
.ab[data-ae="scent-box-clean"].sb-ab.sb-kit .ab-cta,
.ab[data-ae="scent-box-clean"] .ab-cta.sb-k-btn,
.ab[data-ae="scent-box-clean"].sb-ab.sb-kit .ab-cta.sb-k-btn {
  background: var(--ab-ink);
  color: var(--ab-canvas);
  border-color: transparent;
  box-shadow: none;
}

:is(.ab[data-ae="scent-box-clean"] .ab-cta, .ab[data-ae="scent-box-clean"].sb-ab.sb-kit .ab-cta, .ab[data-ae="scent-box-clean"] .ab-cta.sb-k-btn, .ab[data-ae="scent-box-clean"].sb-ab.sb-kit .ab-cta.sb-k-btn):is(:active, .sb-k-pressed, .is-pressed) {
  background: var(--mv-press);
  color: var(--mv-on-press);
  transform: translateY(1px);
  filter: none;
}

@media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) {
  :is(.ab[data-ae="scent-box-clean"] .ab-cta, .ab[data-ae="scent-box-clean"].sb-ab.sb-kit .ab-cta, .ab[data-ae="scent-box-clean"] .ab-cta.sb-k-btn, .ab[data-ae="scent-box-clean"].sb-ab.sb-kit .ab-cta.sb-k-btn):is(:active, .sb-k-pressed, .is-pressed) {
    transform: none;
```

### Actions

#### Primary button (`btn-primary`)

Use: The one main action on a screen.

- States: rest, pressed, focus, disabled
- Glossary terms covered: Button, Control

State: rest

```html
<button type="button" class="ab-cta sb-k-btn">
  <span class="ab-cta-label">Continue</span>
  <span class="ab-cta-arrow" aria-hidden="true"></span>
</button>
```

State: pressed

```html
<button type="button" class="ab-cta sb-k-btn is-pressed sb-k-pressed">
  <span class="ab-cta-label">Continue</span>
  <span class="ab-cta-arrow" aria-hidden="true"></span>
</button>
```

State: focus

```html
<button type="button" class="ab-cta sb-k-btn is-focus sb-k-focus">
  <span class="ab-cta-label">Continue</span>
  <span class="ab-cta-arrow" aria-hidden="true"></span>
</button>
```

State: disabled

```html
<button type="button" class="ab-cta sb-k-btn" disabled="">
  <span class="ab-cta-label">Continue</span>
  <span class="ab-cta-arrow" aria-hidden="true"></span>
</button>
```

#### Secondary button (`btn-secondary`)

Use: The alternative next to a primary button.

- States: rest, pressed, focus, disabled
- Glossary terms covered: Button

State: rest

```html
<button type="button" class="ab-type-apply sb-k-btn2">Not now</button>
```

State: pressed

```html
<button type="button" class="ab-type-apply sb-k-btn2 is-pressed sb-k-pressed">Not now</button>
```

State: focus

```html
<button type="button" class="ab-type-apply sb-k-btn2 is-focus sb-k-focus">Not now</button>
```

State: disabled

```html
<button type="button" class="ab-type-apply sb-k-btn2" disabled="">Not now</button>
```

#### Text button (`btn-text`)

Use: Low-emphasis actions: Skip, Cancel, See all.

- States: rest, pressed
- Glossary terms covered: Button

State: rest

```html
<button type="button" class="sb-k-textbtn">See all</button>
```

State: pressed

```html
<button type="button" class="sb-k-textbtn is-pressed sb-k-pressed">See all</button>
```

#### Destructive button (`btn-destructive`)

Use: Delete, remove, sign out.

- States: rest, pressed
- Glossary terms covered: Button

State: rest

```html
<button type="button" class="sb-k-danger">Delete</button>
```

State: pressed

```html
<button type="button" class="sb-k-danger is-pressed sb-k-pressed">Delete</button>
```

#### Icon button (`btn-icon`)

Use: A single icon action in a top bar or row.

- States: rest, pressed
- Glossary terms covered: Button

State: rest

```html
<button aria-label="Notifications" type="button" class="ab-type-apply sb-icon-btn sb-k-iconbtn">
  <svg class="sb-ico" data-i="bell" width="20" height="20" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.6" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><path d="M6 16V11a6 6 0 0 1 12 0v5l1.5 2h-15z"></path><path d="M10 20.5a2 2 0 0 0 4 0"></path></svg>
</button>
```

State: pressed

```html
<button aria-label="Notifications" type="button" class="ab-type-apply sb-icon-btn sb-k-iconbtn is-pressed sb-k-pressed">
  <svg class="sb-ico" data-i="bell" width="20" height="20" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.6" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><path d="M6 16V11a6 6 0 0 1 12 0v5l1.5 2h-15z"></path><path d="M10 20.5a2 2 0 0 0 4 0"></path></svg>
</button>
```

#### Split button (`btn-split`)

Use: A default action plus a menu of related ones on an attached chevron.

- States: rest, open
- Glossary terms covered: Split Button

State: rest

```html
<div class="sb-k-splitwrap">
  <div class="sb-k-split">
    <button type="button" class="ab-cta sb-k-btn sb-k-split-main">
      <span class="ab-cta-label">Open project</span>
    </button>
    <button aria-label="More actions" aria-haspopup="menu" aria-expanded="false" type="button" class="ab-cta sb-k-split-more">
      <svg class="sb-ico" data-i="down" width="20" height="20" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.6" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><path d="m6 9.5 6 6 6-6"></path></svg>
    </button>
  </div>
</div>
```

State: open


#### Floating button (`fab`)

Use: The screen's main create action, floating above scrolling content.

- States: rest, pressed
- Glossary terms covered: Floating Button

State: rest

```html

  <ul class="ab-list sb-k-list">

        </span>
      </span>
    </li>
    <li class="ab-row sb-k-row" tabindex="0">
      <span class="sb-k-lead">
        <span class="sb-k-av sb-k-av-s" aria-hidden="true">A</span>
      </span>
      <span class="sb-k-text">
        <span class="sb-k-title">Annual report</span>
        <span class="sb-k-sub">Proofs</span>
      </span>
      <span class="sb-k-trail">
        92%
        <span class="sb-k-chev">
          <svg class="sb-ico" data-i="chev" width="16" height="16" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.6" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><path d="m9.5 6 6 6-6 6"></path></svg>
        </span>
      </span>
    </li>
  </ul>
  <button aria-label="New project" type="button" class="ab-cta sb-k-fab">
    <svg class="sb-ico" data-i="plus" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.6" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><path d="M12 5v14M5 12h14"></path></svg>
  </button>
</div>
```

#### Link (`link`)

Use: Text that goes to another page or opens a resource.

- States: rest, pressed, focus
- Glossary terms covered: Link

State: rest

```html
<p class="sb-k-para">
  By continuing you accept the
  <a role="link" tabindex="0" class="sb-k-link">terms of use</a>
  .
</p>
```

State: pressed

```html
<p class="sb-k-para">
  By continuing you accept the
  <a role="link" tabindex="0" class="sb-k-link is-pressed sb-k-pressed">terms of use</a>
  .
</p>
```

State: focus

```html
<p class="sb-k-para">
  By continuing you accept the
  <a role="link" tabindex="0" class="sb-k-link is-focus sb-k-focus">terms of use</a>
  .
</p>
```

#### Icon (`icon`)

Use: One line-icon set at fixed sizes, always paired with a label or an accessible name.

- States: rest
- Glossary terms covered: Icon

State: rest

```html
<div class="sb-k-icons">
  <span class="sb-k-icon">
    <svg class="sb-ico" data-i="search" width="20" height="20" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.6" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><circle cx="11" cy="11" r="6.5"></circle><path d="m16 16 4.5 4.5"></path></svg>
```
